Result: 在 WILDTRACK 数据集上达到 94.3% IDF1 和 93.3% MOTA，性能可与中心化先进方法竞争；在 100 个摄像头规模下维持 30 FPS，跨相机延迟低于 10 ms，通信开销仅 2.2%。

Conclusion: MV3DT 证明了分布式、轻量通信的多视角 3D 跟踪可以兼顾准确性、实时性与可扩展性，是大规模重叠相机网络中实时跟踪的实用方案。

Abstract: Multi-camera tracking with overlapping fields of view typically relies on centralized fusion, which creates computational bottlenecks that prevent deployment at scale. We present MV3DT, a fully distributed framework for real-time multi-view 3D tracking that achieves accurate identity propagation and occlusion recovery through peer-to-peer coordination, eliminating the need for central aggregation. Each camera node executes a lightweight modular pipeline comprising monocular 3D perception, distributed multi-view association, and collaborative fusion via lightweight messaging. MV3DT achieves 94.3% IDF1 and 93.3% MOTA on WILDTRACK, competitive with state-of-the-art centralized methods, while demonstrating superior scalability by sustaining 30 FPS on 100 cameras with less than 10 ms inter-camera latency and only 2.2% communication overhead. MV3DT operates in a zero-shot regime given camera calibrations, requiring no scene-specific learning and making it directly deployable in new environments. These results establish MV3DT as a practical solution for real-time multi-view tracking in large-scale overlapping camera networks.

</details>


### [36] [Cascade Classification of Dermoscopic Images of Skin Neoplasms with Controllable Sensitivity and External Clinical Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13135)
*Elena S. Kozachok,Sergey S. Seregin,Aleksandr V. Kozachok,Ilya P. Latyshev,Oleg I. Samovarov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文比较多种深度学习架构与分类策略在皮肤镜肿瘤图像分类中的表现，发现模型从开放国际数据迁移到独立临床数据时存在明显泛化与校准下降；两阶段级联方案在多数情况下优于单阶段多分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估不同深度学习架构和分类流程在皮肤肿瘤皮肤镜图像上的可迁移性，尤其关注从公开数据集训练后应用到真实临床数据时的性能、敏感性和校准可靠性。

Method: 比较ViT-B/16、Swin-S、ConvNeXt-S、EfficientNetV2-S四种ImageNet预训练模型，在二分类、单阶段四分类、两阶段级联分类三种方案下训练与评估；训练数据来自聚合的ISIC Archive开放数据，并在内部留出集及两个独立临床数据集上测试，同时评估ROC-AUC、敏感性、ECE、macro F1和统计显著性。

Result: 内部二分类ROC-AUC达0.952–0.966，但在Sechenov University临床集上降至0.797–0.893，敏感性降至0.53–0.67，ECE升至0.27–0.39，显示恶性风险低估。临床数据上仅证实ViT-B/16在二分类阶段显著劣于其他架构；分化阶段无架构显著占优。级联方案对多数架构提升macro F1，但仅ViT-B/16达到显著性。ISIC MILK10k上11分类平均类别敏感性为0.525。

Conclusion: 可调二分类分诊阈值可更好控制敏感性，并更贴近临床鉴别诊断流程；然而显著的外部泛化和校准缺口表明，部署前必须进行独立临床验证与重新校准。

Abstract: Purpose. To compare deep learning architectures and classification schemes for dermoscopic images of skin neoplasms and assess their generalization on transfer from open international datasets to independent clinical datasets of Russian practice.
  Methods. Four architectures (ViT-B/16, Swin-S, ConvNeXt-S, EfficientNetV2-S) were compared in three schemes: binary (malignant/benign), single-stage four-class (benign, MEL, SCC, BCC), and a two-stage cascade (binary triage, then three-class differentiation MEL/SCC/BCC). All models used ImageNet-pretrained weights and a single augmentation protocol on aggregated open ISIC Archive data, and were evaluated on an internal held-out sample and two clinical datasets (Melanoscope AI mobile system; Sechenov University).
  Results. Internally the binary stage attains ROC-AUC 0.952-0.966; on Sechenov University it drops to 0.797-0.893, sensitivity to 0.53-0.67, and ECE rises from 0.02 to 0.27-0.39 with underestimation of malignancy, quantifying a generalization gap in ranking and calibration. Paired tests confirm one inter-architecture result on clinical data: the deficit of ViT-B/16 at the binary stage (p<0.05); at the differentiation stage no architecture has a proven advantage. The cascade raises macro F1 over single-stage four-class classification for most architectures, but significantly only for ViT-B/16, by recovering malignant lesions assigned to the dominant benign class. On ISIC MILK10k, direct 11-class classification yields mean-class sensitivity 0.525.
  Conclusion. A tunable triage threshold gives sensitivity control not attainable in standard single-stage (argmax) classification and better reproduces clinical differential-diagnosis logic. The persistent generalization gap mandates external clinical validation and recalibration before deployment.

</details>


### [37] [An Extensible and Lightweight Unified Architecture for Demosaicing Pixel-bin Image Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13136)
*Saurabh Kumar,Nutan Sairam Yenneti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向多种像素合并（pixel-bin）图像传感器的统一、模块化、轻量级去马赛克架构，并加入无需学习的CFA识别模块，实现即插即用与更高图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机相机越来越多采用pixel-bin传感器，以在分辨率和进光量之间取得折中。但这类传感器相较传统Bayer CFA具有更大的颜色间隔，导致去马赛克更困难。同时，现有深度学习去马赛克方法通常依赖特定CFA，需要为不同传感器维护多个模型，增加端侧存储、计算和开发维护成本。

Method: 作者设计了一个模块化统一去马赛克网络，用于适配多种pixel-bin CFA模式；该架构强调可扩展性与轻量化。为支持自动化流程，还提出一个无需训练的CFA识别模块，用于从raw数据中准确判断CFA类型，从而选择或配置相应的去马赛克处理。

Result: 摘要声称该方法在多种pixel-bin传感器上可提供更高图像质量，同时减少对多个CFA专用模型的依赖，并具备较好的端侧资源友好性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工作针对pixel-bin传感器去马赛克的通用化与部署难题，提出统一架构和学习-free CFA识别机制，有望降低智能手机成像管线中多CFA适配的复杂度。

Abstract: Pixel-bin image sensors are becoming the default choice for smartphone cameras due to their resolution vs light-gathering trade-off. However, their larger inter-color separation compared to the Bayer color filter array (CFA) makes them challenging to demosaic. Furthermore, existing deep learning-based demosaicing methods are CFA-specific, requiring multiple individual models that take up precious onboard resources and demand larger development and maintenance efforts. In this work, we propose a modular unified architecture for demosaicing various pixel-bin sensors that provides higher image quality while being extensible and lightweight. Additionally, to enable plug-and-play operation, we introduce a learning-free CFA-identification module to detect the CFA type of raw data accurately.

</details>


### [38] [Iterative Visual Thinking: Teaching Vision-Language Models Spatial Self-Correction through Visual Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13156)
*Animesh Tripathy,Aswanth Krishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代视觉思考（IVT），让视觉语言模型通过观察自己预测的框并逐步修正，从而学习空间定位的自我纠错能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在单次空间定位上表现较强，但缺乏观察并修正自身预测的机制；直接让模型基于渲染出的预测结果进行迭代反而会导致性能大幅下降，说明定位能力与自我纠错能力之间存在明显缺口。

Method: 提出闭环框架IVT：模型先预测边界框，再在图像上观察渲染后的预测结果，并基于视觉反馈迭代细化。训练采用两阶段方案：第一阶段利用基础模型自身预测作为真实错误样本，由教师VLM生成纠错推理轨迹，构造无需人工标注的监督微调数据；第二阶段使用带IoU奖励的Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）稳定多步细化过程。

Result: 在RefCOCOg、Ref-Adv和Ref-L4组成的505个测试样本混合基准上，IVT的SFT warm-up超过单次预测基础模型：Acc@0.5从79.6%提升到82.0%，Acc@0.7提升到74.1%，Acc@0.9提升到48.3%。GRPO进一步将逐步迭代中的IoU退化降低5倍。

Conclusion: 空间自我纠错并非视觉语言模型天然具备的能力，但可以通过少量训练样本和闭环视觉反馈机制学习获得；IVT证明了在 modest scale 下提升多步空间定位稳定性和精度的可行性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong singleshot spatial grounding, yet lack any mechanism to observe and correct their own predictions. We find that naively prompting a VLM to iterate over rendered visualizations of its predictions causes catastrophic failure: Acc@0.5 on referring expression comprehension collapses from 79.6% to 48.7% (a 31 percentage point drop), revealing a fundamental gap between grounding capability and self-correction ability. We propose Iterative Visual Thinking (IVT), a closed-loop framework in which the model predicts a bounding box, observes the prediction rendered on the image, and iteratively refines through visual feedback. A two-phase training recipe closes the self-correction gap: first, we exploit the base model's own predictions as realistic errors and prompt a teacher VLM to generate corrective reasoning traces, yielding supervised data without human annotation; second, we apply Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a simple IoU reward to stabilize multi-step refinement. On a mixed benchmark spanning RefCOCOg, Ref-Adv, and Ref-L4 (505 test samples), SFT warm-up with IVT surpasses the single-shot base model on every metric: Acc@0.5 rises to 82.0% (+2.4pp), Acc@0.7 to 74.1% (+3.2pp), and Acc@0.9 to 48.3% (+2.8pp). GRPO further reduces per-step IoU degradation by 5x, stabilizing the refinement trajectory. All training uses only 2,400 samples on a single GPU, demonstrating that spatial self-correction is a learnable capability that can be instilled at modest scale.

</details>


### [39] [Transformer-Guided Graph Attention for Direct Cardiac Mesh Reconstruction: A Structural Digital Twin Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13188)
*Abhishek H S,Akash Ganamukhi,Abhimanyu Suresh,Aditya G Hiremath,Prasad B Honnavalli,Adithya Balasubramanyam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从原始3D心脏CT/MRI图像直接生成平滑、可仿真的患者特异性心脏表面网格的端到端方法，绕过传统分割、Marching Cubes与人工清理流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者特异性心脏建模对精准心脏医学很重要，但传统网格生成流程耗时、依赖人工、操作者差异大且需要专业知识，限制了临床落地。

Method: 采用3D Swin Transformer编码器-解码器从CT/MRI体数据中提取体素特征，并结合Graph Attention Network头部迭代变形模板网格，使其贴合患者心脏边界，实现从原始图像到表面网格的单次前向推理。

Result: 在MM-WHS 2017基准上测试，CT Dice为0.84，MRI Dice为0.83；网格平均Chamfer距离为1.8 mm，95百分位表面距离低于5 mm；无需Marching Cubes、平滑滤波或人工清理。

Conclusion: 该方法强调心脏数字孪生流程中几何保真度和拓扑正确性比单纯像素级Dice更关键，通过消除后处理瓶颈提升了患者特异性心脏仿真进入临床应用的可及性。

Abstract: Building patient-specific cardiac models sits at the heart of precision cardiology, yet getting those models into clinical use keeps running into the same wall: mesh generation is slow, messy, and frustrating. The standard workflow -- segmenting the image, running Marching Cubes, and then manually cleaning up the result -- is time-consuming, inconsistent across operators, and demands specialist knowledge most clinical teams do not have. We take a fundamentally different approach. Instead of treating segmentation and mesh generation as two separate problems, we train a single end-to-end network that goes directly from a raw 3D medical image to a smooth, simulation-ready cardiac surface mesh. The core is a 3D Swin Transformer encoder-decoder that extracts volumetric features from CT or MRI volumes, paired with a Graph Attention Network (GAT) head that iteratively deforms a template mesh to fit the patient's cardiac boundary. We tested on the MM-WHS 2017 benchmark using both CT and MRI. Segmentation scores were competitive (Dice of 0.84 on CT, 0.83 on MRI), but the primary focus is mesh quality: mean Chamfer distance of 1.8 mm, with 95th-percentile surface distance below 5 mm. Every mesh is produced in a single forward pass -- no Marching Cubes, no smoothing filters, no manual cleanup. We argue that for cardiac digital twin pipelines, geometric fidelity and topological correctness matter more than pixel-level Dice scores. By removing the post-processing bottleneck, this approach makes patient-specific cardiac simulation substantially more accessible for clinical use.

</details>


### [40] [Visual Place Recognition in Forests with Depth-Aware Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13206)
*Walter Nedov,Saimunur Rahman,Kavindie Katuwandeniya,David Hall,Kaushik Roy,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级深度感知蒸馏框架，将深度几何信息注入基于 DINOv2 的视觉地点识别模型，在保持预训练描述子空间的同时提升自然森林场景中的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然森林环境中的视觉地点识别面临植被重复、结构线索弱、跨次遍历外观变化显著等问题；仅依赖外观特征的模型容易受到光照、季节和植被状态变化影响，因此需要引入更稳定的几何线索。

Method: 方法采用深度感知蒸馏框架，将深度信息作为互补几何模态注入 DINOv2 基础的地点识别模型，同时尽量保持其已有的预训练描述子空间，以实现轻量化且兼容现有表征的增强。

Result: 在 WildCross 基准上，该方法相较于仅使用外观信息的对应模型取得性能提升，并表现出对外观变化更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，深度信息是自然环境地点识别中的强互补模态；深度感知蒸馏是提升森林感知与地点识别鲁棒性的有前景方向。

Abstract: Visual place recognition in natural forest environments remains challenging due to repetitive vegetation, weak structural cues, and significant appearance variation across traversals. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a lightweight depth-aware distillation framework that injects geometric cues into a DINOv2-based place recognition model, while maintaining its pre-trained descriptor space. Evaluated on the recent WildCross benchmark, the proposed approach yields gains over an appearance-only counterpart, providing robustness to appearance variations. These results demonstrate the importance of depth as a strong complementary modality for place recognition in natural environments and identify depth-aware distillation as a promising direction for more robust forest perception.

</details>


### [41] [TimeLens: On-Device Artifact Recognition with Retrieval-Augmented Question Answering for the Grand Egyptian Museum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13267)
*Rawan Hesham,Ali Ashraf,Amr Ahmed,Malak Alaa,Omar Ahmed,Omar Wagih*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TimeLens 是面向 Grand Egyptian Museum 的 AI 双语移动导览应用，结合端侧实时文物检测与双语 RAG 问答，在移动端性能、识别精度和事实可靠性之间取得工程化平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在解决馆内 AI 导览的三个关键问题：相似文物之间的细粒度识别困难、 curated 训练数据与游客手持拍摄条件之间的域差异，以及生成式导览可能产生无依据历史陈述的风险。

Method: 方法包括两部分：一是通过数据质量驱动的迭代流程训练端侧 YOLOv8n 文物检测器，流程从 YOLO-World 自动标注、空间规则清洗到全人工标注，以验证标签质量对性能的决定性作用；二是构建基于 108 条记录 ChromaDB 知识库的双语 RAG 系统，对 7 个候选语言模型进行评测并选择 Gemma 4 E2B（Q4 K M），同时进行 10 项延迟优化。系统最终集成于 Flutter 移动应用，支持双语界面、位置门控和文本转语音。

Result: 最终端侧检测模型大小为 5.97 MB，能够在中端手机上实时运行，并达到 mAP@0.5 = 0.995、mAP@0.5:0.95 = 0.924，且解决了此前所有失败类别。RAG 子系统在优化后将端到端延迟从超过 30 秒降低到约 10 秒。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，高质量人工标注对细粒度馆内文物识别至关重要；结合轻量级端侧检测与知识库约束的 RAG，可以构建较高精度、较低延迟且更可控的双语博物馆 AI 导览系统。

Abstract: TimeLens is an AI-powered bilingual mobile guide for the Grand Egyptian Museum (GEM). Pointing a phone at an exhibit, a visitor sees the artifact recognized in real time and can ask follow-up questions answered in English or Arabic. The work addresses three problems specific to in-gallery deployment: fine-grained visual similarity among 51 catalogued artifacts (many near-identical Ramesside statues), the gap between curated training data and handheld camera conditions, and the risk of an AI guide stating unsupported historical facts. Two engineering contributions are reported. First, an on-device artifact detector was developed through a data-quality-driven iteration study -- from foundation-model auto-annotation (YOLO-World), through spatial label-cleaning rules, to a fully hand-annotated dataset -- isolating label quality as the decisive factor: the final YOLOv8n model resolves every previously failing class while remaining a 5.97 MB TensorFlow Lite asset that runs in real time on a mid-range phone (mAP@0.5 = 0.995, mAP@0.5:0.95 = 0.924). Second, a bilingual Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) guide, grounded in a 108-record ChromaDB knowledge base, was benchmarked across seven candidate language models, with Gemma 4 E2B (Q4 K M) selected; ten targeted optimizations reduce end-to-end latency from over 30 s to approximately 10 s. Both subsystems are integrated in a production Flutter application with bilingual interface, museum location gating, and text-to-speech support.

</details>


### [42] [Zero-Shot Captioning for Cultural Heritage: Automated Image Analysis of Traditional Indonesian Clothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13275)
*Anugrah Aidin Yotolembah,Novanto Yudistira,Gembong Edhi Setyawan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Custom ZeroCLIP，用检索增强的视觉语言框架在未见省份上生成印尼传统服饰图像描述，并在多项指标与人工评估中优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对低资源文化遗产场景中传统服饰图像描述数据稀缺、文化词汇难以准确生成、模型对未见地域泛化能力不足的问题，探索零样本条件下的文化语义描述生成。

Method: 构建包含 38 个省份、3,800 张专家标注图像的数据集；采用省份级归纳式零样本划分：24 个已见省份训练、6 个已见省份验证、8 个未见省份测试。模型结合冻结的 CLIP ViT-B/32 图像编码器、CLIP 文本编码器、BERT 文本编码器和 LSTM 描述解码器；推理时仅从训练省份描述中检索辅助信息，不使用未见省份的图像、标签或描述。

Result: Custom ZeroCLIP 达到 CLIPScore 0.8536、BLEU-4 0.3342、METEOR 0.4859，优于现有基线。消融实验显示检索机制带来 19.3% 的 METEOR 提升，并改善文化词汇恢复；人工评估也表明其文化准确性和流畅性更强。

Conclusion: 检索增强的领域适配方法能够有效提升低资源文化遗产场景中的零样本图像描述生成能力，尤其有助于恢复文化相关词汇并提高对未见地域服饰的泛化表现。

Abstract: This paper presents Custom ZeroCLIP, a retrieval-augmented vision-language framework for zero-shot captioning of Indonesian traditional garments. The dataset contains 3,800 expert-annotated images from all 38 Indonesian provinces. Using a province-level inductive zero-shot protocol, the model is trained on 24 seen provinces, validated on 6 seen provinces, and evaluated on 8 unseen provinces. The framework combines a frozen CLIP ViT-B/32 image encoder, a CLIP text encoder, a BERT text encoder, and an LSTM caption decoder. During inference, unseen-province labels and captions are unavailable, and retrieval uses only captions from training provinces. No unseen-province image, label, or caption is used during training, validation, or retrieval-bank construction. Custom ZeroCLIP achieves a CLIPScore of 0.8536, BLEU-4 of 0.3342, and METEOR of 0.4859, outperforming existing baselines. Ablation results show that retrieval improves cultural vocabulary recovery with a 19.3\% METEOR gain, while human evaluation confirms stronger cultural accuracy and fluency. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of retrieval-augmented domain adaptation for culturally grounded caption generation in low-resource heritage settings. The dataset is publicly available at https://github.com/AnugrahAidinYotolembah/Traditional-Indonesian-Clothing-Captioning-Dataset.

</details>


### [43] [Cross-Modal Masked Compositional Concept Modeling for Enhancing Visio-Linguistic Compositionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13288)
*Wei Li,Zhen Huang,Xinmei Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MACCO，通过跨模态遮蔽并重建组合概念来增强视觉语言模型的组合理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP 等对比学习视觉语言模型虽能学习强大的图文联合表示，但在组合理解上存在明显不足，常表现出“词袋”行为，难以准确建模对象关系、属性-对象绑定和词序依赖。其原因包括过度依赖全局单向量表示，以及未充分利用图文配对数据中天然存在的丰富组合信息。

Method: 提出 MACCO（Masked Compositional Concept Modeling）框架：在一个模态中遮蔽组合概念，并利用另一模态的完整上下文信息进行重建，从而促使模型捕获并对齐跨模态组合结构。同时引入两个辅助目标，对遮蔽特征进行跨模态对齐与模态内正则化，以提升表示的一致性和结构性。

Result: 在五个组合性基准上的大量实验和深入分析表明，该方法显著提升了视觉语言模型的组合能力，并增强了模型捕获句法结构和语言信息的能力。此外，组合性的提升还对文本到图像生成和多模态大语言模型产生积极影响。

Conclusion: MACCO 通过显式建模和重建跨模态组合概念，有效缓解了现有视觉语言模型的词袋化问题，为提升图文表示的结构化理解、生成任务和多模态推理能力提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Contrastively trained vision-language models like CLIP, have made remarkable progress in learning joint image-text representations, but still face challenges in compositional understanding. They often exhibit a "bag-of-words" behavior--struggling to capture the object relations, attribute-object bindings, and word order dependencies. This limitation arises not only from the reliance on global, single-vector representations for optimization, but also from the insufficient exploitation and modeling of the rich compositional information inherently present in paired image text data. In this work, we propose MACCO (MAsked Compositional Concept MOdeling), a framework that masks compositional concepts in one modality and reconstructs them conditioned on the full contextual information from the other, enabling the model to capture and align cross-modal compositional structures more effectively. To facilitate this process, we introduce two auxiliary objectives that jointly align and regularize masked features both inter-modally and intra-modally. Extensive experiments on five compositional benchmarks, along with in-depth analyses, demonstrate that our approach not only significantly enhances compositionality in VLMs but also improves their ability to capture syntactic structure and linguistic information. Additionally, the improved compositionality also benefits text-to-image generation and multimodal large language model. Code is available at https://github.com/hiker-lw/MACCO.

</details>


### [44] [HYDRA-X: Native Unified Multimodal Models with Holistic Visual Tokenizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13289)
*Guozhen Zhang,Xuerui Qiu,Yutao Cui,Tianhui Song,Changlin Li,Junzhe Li,Tao Huang,Xiao Zhang,Yang Li,Jianbing Wu,Miles Yang,Zhao Zhong,Liefeng Bo,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HYDRA-X 提出一种在单一 ViT 内统一图像与视频 tokenization 的整体视觉 tokenizer，并将其用于统一多模态模型，在理解、生成与编辑任务上取得较强表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型需要将图像和视频等不同视觉输入映射到统一表示空间，但同时实现高效时空重建能力与图像/视频语义感知仍具挑战；此外，编辑流程中源-目标交互位置也影响一致性与训练收敛。

Method: 作者设计了基于单一 Vision Transformer 的图像-视频统一 tokenizer。通过消融发现：帧级因果时间注意力足以支持视觉重建，而完整时空注意力会降低重建质量；层级式时间压缩优于单步压缩。为增强语义结构，提出轻量级 decompressor，在图像-视频教师联合监督下对时间压缩特征进行上采样。进一步将编辑中的源-目标交互放在 tokenizer 的 latent 层，而非 LLM 的语义层。

Result: 在 7B dense 模型配置下，HYDRA-X 在图像与视频理解、生成以及编辑相关任务中表现强劲；latent-level 源-目标交互提升了编辑一致性并加速收敛。

Conclusion: HYDRA-X 表明，统一图像和视频 tokenization 可以在单一 ViT 框架内实现，并且合理的时间注意力、层级压缩和联合语义监督对构建高效统一视觉 tokenizer 至关重要，为未来统一 tokenizer 的 UMM 发展提供了方向。

Abstract: Holistic visual tokenizers are fundamental to unified multimodal models (UMMs) as they map diverse visual inputs into a unified representation space. In this paper, we present HYDRA-X, the first UMM that unifies image and video tokenization within a single Vision Transformer (ViT). Our design is driven by two core challenges: efficiently injecting spatiotemporal reconstruction capability into a native ViT, and embedding image- and video-level semantic awareness into the latent space. To address the first, comprehensive ablations reveal two key findings: (1) frame-level causal temporal attention suffices for visual reconstruction, whereas full spatiotemporal attention degrades it; and (2) hierarchical temporal compression substantially outperforms single-step alternatives. To tackle the second, we propose a lightweight decompressor that upsamples temporally compressed features under joint image-video teacher supervision, thereby enforcing complementary semantic structures within the compact latent space. Building on this holistic tokenizer, we further propose a principled improvement of the editing pipeline: source-target interaction should occur at the latent level inside the tokenizer rather than at the semantic level inside the LLM, substantially improving editing consistency and accelerating convergence. Instantiated at the 7B dense model, HYDRA-X achieves strong performance across image and video understanding and generation tasks, paving the way for future unified-tokenizer UMMs.

</details>


### [45] [DuET: Dual Expert Trajectories for Diffusion Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13303)
*Lidia Troeshestova,Alexander Ustyuzhanin,Sergey Kastryulin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DuET 是一种无需训练的扩散图像编辑推理方法，通过在去噪过程中短暂切换到文生图轨迹，再回到图像编辑轨迹，以提升复杂或大幅度编辑的执行效果与自然度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于指令的扩散编辑方法在每个去噪步骤都持续依赖源图像条件。虽然这有助于保持结构，但当目标场景与输入图像差异较大时，过强的源图像约束会限制编辑完成度，并导致结果不够自然。

Method: 提出 DuET（Dual Expert Trajectories），一种训练-free 的推理策略。它在采样过程中临时放松源图像条件，使轨迹进入文生图阶段以靠近目标文本分布，随后再切回图像条件编辑模式，从而兼顾目标语义迁移与源图结构约束。该方法不修改模型权重，也不增加采样成本。

Result: 在多种模型和基准测试上，DuET 稳定提升了指令相关性、语义一致性和感知质量。对于目标与源图差异较大的编辑任务，效果尤其明显。

Conclusion: DuET 表明，在扩散编辑中适度、阶段性地放松源图像条件可以改善编辑保真度和视觉自然度。不过，该方法可能带来源图保持度的小幅下降，体现出源图保留与编辑忠实度之间的可预测权衡。

Abstract: Recent diffusion editors perform diverse instruction-based edits while conditioning on the source image at every denoising step. Yet persistent source-image conditioning can limit how fully an edit is executed and how natural the result appears, especially when the target scene diverges substantially from the input. We introduce DuET (Dual Expert Trajectories), a training-free inference method that temporarily relaxes source-image conditioning by transitioning through a text-to-image phase before returning to edit mode, allowing the denoising trajectory to move toward the target distribution while retaining the structural benefits of image-conditioned editing. Without modifying model weights or increasing sampling cost, DuET consistently improves instruction relevance, semantic fidelity, and perceptual quality across diverse models and benchmarks. In some cases, these gains come with a modest reduction in source-image preservation, revealing a predictable trade-off between source preservation and edit fidelity.

</details>


### [46] [ReFree: Towards Realistic Co-Speech Video Generation via Reward-Free RL and Multilevel Speech Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13304)
*Salaheldin Mohamed,M. Hamza Mughal,Rishabh Dabral,Christian Theobalt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReFree-S2V 是一种基于流匹配与预训练视频生成模型的语音驱动肖像动画框架，旨在同时提升唇形同步精度、表情表现力和头部运动自然性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音驱动说话人动画方法往往在精确音素-唇形同步与丰富表情/头部运动之间存在权衡：同步准确的方法容易显得僵硬，而表现力强的方法又可能唇形不准。论文希望解决这一兼顾精细发音与自然表达的问题。

Method: 方法提出 ReFree-S2V：在预训练视频生成模型基础上构建流匹配 speech-to-portrait animation 框架；设计多层级语音表示，同时捕获局部与全局粒度的音素和韵律信息；通过可学习的 level selectors 将不同层级语音特征选择性注入 Transformer blocks；同时引入 reward-free reinforcement learning 到流匹配训练中，以抑制感知上不自然的头部运动，且不依赖手工同步指标、奖励模型或人工偏好标注。

Result: 大量实验表明，ReFree-S2V 在定量唇形同步指标和定性人工评估中的自然度、表现力方面均达到 state-of-the-art，并显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，多层级语音条件建模与选择性特征注入有助于同时提升唇形同步和表情动态，而无需奖励模型的强化学习机制可进一步改善头部运动自然性，是语音驱动肖像动画的一种有效方案。

Abstract: Speech-driven talking character animation seeks to generate life-like portrait videos that convey natural conversation behavior, aligning facial motion with spoken audio. Although recent advances in video generation have substantially improved realism in video-based animation, achieving both accurate lip articulation and expressive behavior remains challenging. Existing approaches typically trade off precise phoneme-to-lip synchronization against dynamic facial expressions and head motion, yielding animations that are either accurate yet rigid, or expressive but poorly synchronized. We address this challenge by proposing ReFree-S2V, a flow-matching speech-to-portrait animation framework that builds upon a pretrained video generation model to achieve fine-grained speech articulation and high-level expressive cues in speech-driven portrait animation. This model introduces a multi-level speech representation capturing phonetic and prosodic information at both local and global granularities. These representations are selectively injected into transformer blocks via learnable level selectors, enabling both accurate lip synchronization and natural expressive motion. To achieve natural head movements, we further introduce a novel reward-free reinforcement learning scheme into flow-matching training to discourage perceptually implausible motion without relying on handcrafted synchronization metrics or reward models, or the high cost of human preference annotation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReFree-S2V achieves state-of-the-art performance, significantly outperforming existing methods in both quantitative lip-sync accuracy and qualitative human evaluations of naturalness and expressivity.

</details>


### [47] [MagPlus: Bridging Micro-to-Regular Facial Expressions through Learnable Magnification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13312)
*Sliman Jammal,Andrei Sharf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MagPlus 提出一种无需在微表情数据上重训生成模型的可迁移处理流程，通过放大微弱微表情运动，使其可被现有宏表情动画模型处理，再用 DeMagPlus 将生成结果恢复到真实微表情强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微表情具有幅度小、持续时间短、标注数据稀缺等特点，导致直接建模与生成困难；现有方法常存在生成质量有限、鲁棒性不足、泛化能力差的问题。作者希望利用已有成熟的标准面部表情动画模型来缓解微表情数据不足带来的训练瓶颈。

Method: 方法包括两个核心模块：MagPlus 将微表情中的细微面部运动放大到普通表情模型可识别和处理的运动范围，使其兼容预训练宏表情模型；随后标准面部动画模型执行迁移或合成等任务；DeMagPlus 再将生成的较强运动还原到合理的微表情强度，同时保留生成的时序动态。实验中该流程被接入 FOMM、FSRT、MetaPortrait 和 EmoPortraits 四种未使用微表情数据训练的模型。

Result: 实验表明，MagPlus-DeMagPlus 能使未在微表情数据上训练过的宏表情动画模型生成更真实的微表情运动，并且无需重新训练这些主干模型。

Conclusion: 该论文的核心贡献在于将微表情生成问题转化为“运动放大—标准模型处理—运动还原”的可迁移流程，从而提高微表情合成的质量、鲁棒性与跨模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Facial micro-expressions are subtle and short-lived facial movements that provide important cues about genuine human emotions. However, modeling and generating them remains difficult because annotated micro-expression data is limited and the underlying facial motions are extremely weak. Existing micro-expression generation methods therefore often suffer from limited quality, weak robustness, and poor generalization. We propose MagPlus, a transferable micro-expression processing pipeline that connects micro-expression analysis with standard facial animation models. Instead of training a dedicated generator from scratch, MagPlus learns to magnify subtle facial motions into the range of regular facial expressions, transforming micro-expressions into signals that are compatible with existing facial expression processing models. The magnified sequence is then used by a standard facial expression model for tasks such as transfer and synthesis. A complementary DeMagPlus module then restores the generated motion back to realistic micro-expression intensity levels while preserving the synthesized dynamics. We evaluate the framework using four facial animation models: FOMM, FSRT, MetaPortrait, and EmoPortraits. None of these models are trained on micro-expression data. Experiments show that MagPlus-DeMagPlus enables pretrained macro-expression models to generate more realistic micro-expression motion without retraining the backbones.

</details>


### [48] [Masked and Predictive Self-Supervised Foundation Models for 3D Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13315)
*Esra Ergün,Hersh Chandarana,Dan Sodickson,Gözde Ünal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了两类MRI疾病检测的自监督预训练范式：基于重建的MAE与基于预测表征的JEPA，并发现带频谱域监督的MAE在多个下游任务中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MRI基础模型研究多集中于分割和密集预测任务，对MRI疾病检测场景下自监督基础模型的系统性研究不足；同时，不同自监督目标如何影响下游疾病检测性能仍缺乏清晰认识。

Method: 作者比较MAE与JEPA两种预训练范式。在MAE中引入新的频谱域重建损失，以增强模型对细粒度解剖结构的敏感性；在JEPA中加入方差—协方差正则化（VCR），促进潜在表征去相关。模型在异质单对比MRI体数据上进行对比无关预训练，不采用多模态拼接，并在五个下游疾病检测任务上评估。

Result: 实验表明，自监督目标设计显著影响下游疾病检测效果。频谱正则化在疾病判别信号具有强高频解剖结构时提升最大；协方差正则化在判别信息分布于多个去相关特征维度时更有益。总体上，带频谱域监督的MAE在MRI疾病检测中表现最稳定且性能最佳。

Conclusion: 医学影像自监督目标会编码特定归纳偏置，其下游收益取决于任务结构。针对疾病检测任务，频谱域监督的MAE更适合捕获细粒度解剖异常，是MRI基础模型预训练中较有前景的目标设计。

Abstract: Self-supervised foundation models have shown strong promise in medical imaging. However, existing MRI foundation-model studies have primarily emphasized segmentation and dense prediction tasks, while systematic investigation of self-supervised foundation models for MRI-based disease detection remains limited. In this work, we investigate two major self-supervised pretraining paradigms for MRI-based disease detection: reconstruction-based learning via Masked Autoencoders (MAE) and predictive representation learning via Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA). We study the role of auxiliary objectives by introducing a novel spectral-domain reconstruction loss for MAE to enhance sensitivity to fine-grained anatomical structure, and by integrating variance--covariance regularization (VCR) within our JEPA framework to encourage decorrelated latent representations. Our models are pretrained on heterogeneous single-contrast MRI volumes in a contrast-agnostic setting, without modality concatenation. Across five downstream disease detection tasks, our results highlight the importance of self-supervised objective design for medical foundation model pretraining, demonstrating that the downstream benefit of each objective is determined by its relevance to the task's structure. Specifically, spectral regularization yields the largest improvements when the downstream discriminative signal is characterized by strong high-frequency anatomical structures, while covariance regularization is most beneficial when discriminative information spans multiple decorrelated feature dimensions. MAE with spectral-domain supervision consistently achieves superior downstream performance for MRI-based disease detection. These findings suggest that self-supervised objectives in medical imaging encode specific biases, and their downstream benefit is fundamentally conditioned on the task's structure.

</details>


### [49] [OR-Action: Multi-Role Video Understanding with Fine-Grained Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13332)
*Felix Tristram,Ege Özsoy,Christian Benz,Marcel Walch,Ghazal Ghazaei,Nassir Navab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出首个面向手术室细粒度动作理解的动作中心基准，并发现现有场景图方法难以建模时间结构；其提出的纯视觉时序模型及多视角到单视角特征对齐策略取得更优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术室活动理解可支持工作流感知辅助，但场景复杂、遮挡多、传感受限。现有方法多依赖逐帧场景图表示交互关系，难以将帧级关系预测转化为具有时间延展性的细粒度动作，因此需要一个面向时间动作理解的系统评测基准。

Method: 作者基于公开的自我—外部视角手术室数据集，定义细粒度、多角色动作分类体系，并通过从真实场景图状态变化中蒸馏生成密集动作片段，构建动作中心 benchmark。同时比较现有场景图预测方法、加入图神经网络的显式时序建模方法，并提出纯视觉时序模型；进一步设计多视角到单视角的特征对齐策略以提升单视角多角色动作识别。

Result: 实验显示，当前场景图预测方法即便加入图神经网络，也难以有效建模手术室活动的时间结构。所提出的纯视觉时序模型在使用所有可用第一视角视频时显著优于图方法；多到单视角特征对齐策略也提升了单视角动作识别性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为手术室细粒度动作识别提供了首个动作中心评测框架，并表明直接的视觉时序建模比基于场景图的关系建模更适合当前任务；特征对齐方法可降低对大规模第一视角视频采集的依赖。

Abstract: Fine-grained understanding of operating room (OR) activity could enable workflow-aware assistance, yet remains difficult due to clutter, occlusions, and limited sensing. The prevailing approach to model this environment is scene graphs as an interpretable representation of OR interactions. Converting their frame-wise relational predictions into temporally extended, fine-grained actions however, is challenging without explicit temporal modeling. To enable a principled temporal evaluation of current OR understanding methods, we introduce the first action-centric benchmark built on a publicly available ego-exocentric OR dataset by defining a fine-grained, multi-role action taxonomy and generating dense action segments via distillation from ground-truth scene graph state changes. Experiments on this benchmark show that current scene graph prediction methods struggle to model temporal structure, even when adding explicit modeling through Graph Neural Networks. We therefore introduce a vision-only temporal model that outperforms graph-based methods significantly when using all available egocentric video as input. Building on this model we also introduce a novel multi- to single-view feature alignment strategy that improves single-view performance on multi-role action recognition, mitigating the need for extensive egocentric video capture. Benchmark and code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [50] [Dual-Domain Equivariant Generative Adversarial Network for Multimodal CT-PET Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13341)
*Gabriel Steele,Alzahra Altalib,Alessandro Perelli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 DDE-GAN，用于多模态 CT-PET 图像合成，通过联合空间域与频率域学习，并引入旋转等变约束，以提升结构保真度、合成质量和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于 GAN 的 CT-PET 合成方法通常只在空间域建模，容易忽略频谱信息和几何一致性，导致生成图像的解剖结构保真度不足、对旋转等几何变换不够稳定。

Method: 方法构建双域等变生成对抗网络：同时在空间域和 Fourier 频率域学习互补的解剖与频谱特征；在生成器和判别器损失中加入旋转等变约束，使模型在旋转变换下保持一致响应；采用层次化双域训练策略，通过多阶段损失约束域内一致性与域间一致性。

Result: 在 HECKTOR 2022 CT-PET 数据集上的实验表明，DDE-GAN 相比基线模型取得更优的 CT-PET 图像合成质量，尤其在结构准确性和鲁棒性方面表现更好。

Conclusion: 双域学习与几何等变性结合能够显著提升多模态医学图像合成的准确性和稳定性，具备应用于 PET 补全和数据增强的潜力。

Abstract: We present a Dual-Domain Equivariant Generative Adversarial Network (DDE-GAN) for multimodal CT-PET image synthesis. Traditional GAN-based approaches often operate solely in the spatial domain and ignore geometric consistency, resulting in limited structural fidelity. DDE-GAN addresses these challenges by jointly learning from both spatial and frequency (Fourier) domains, capturing complementary anatomical and spectral information. Furthermore, rotational equivariance embedded in the physics of the CT and PET measurements are integrated into the loss of both the generator and discriminator to ensure consistent responses under rotations, improving anatomical accuracy. A hierarchical dual-domain training strategy enforces intra- and inter-domain consistency through multi-stage loss functions. Evaluated on the HECKTOR 2022 CT-PET dataset, DDE-GAN achieves superior synthesis quality over baseline models for CT-PET image synthesis. The results demonstrate that combining dual-domain learning with geometric equivariance substantially enhances multimodal image synthesis accuracy and robustness, enabling practical applications in PET completion and data augmentation.

</details>


### [51] [JointEdit3D: Feed-Forward 3D Scene Editing in a Unified Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13345)
*Xinnan Zhu,Ruijie Xu,Jiayu Ying,Daoguo Dong,Jiachen Xu,Yuan Xie,Xin Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JointEdit3D 是一个前馈式 3D 场景编辑框架，通过联合 RGB 外观生成与几何预测，在单个编辑后 RGB 参考视图条件下生成一致的多视图结果和编辑后几何，并提出配套数据集与 benchmark。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 场景编辑方法通常依赖逐场景优化或先编辑再重建的级联流程，导致测试时开销高、3D 感知不足，并容易产生结构不一致。论文希望在编辑过程中显式耦合外观合成与几何预测，以提升效率和三维一致性。

Method: 方法基于统一的 RGB-geometry 重建-生成潜空间，并将其适配为前馈式 3D 场景编辑框架 JointEdit3D。其核心是非对称潜空间修复：仅观察单个编辑后的 RGB 参考潜变量，在源场景锚定条件下生成其余 RGB 视图和编辑后的几何潜变量。同时引入 SceneAnchor Branch 注入源场景结构信息，并使用编辑/背景感知损失来平衡编辑区域质量与未编辑区域保持。

Result: 实验表明，相比已有基线，JointEdit3D 在编辑区域生成质量和 3D 结构完整性方面取得提升，同时在背景内容保持方面仍具有竞争力。论文还构建了 SceneEdit3D-15K 数据集和 SceneEdit3D-Bench 基准以支持标准化评估。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种更高效且更具 3D 一致性的场景编辑范式，证明联合建模 RGB 外观与几何潜空间有助于解决传统方法中的高成本和结构不一致问题，并通过新数据集推动 3D 场景编辑的系统评测。

Abstract: Existing 3D scene editing methods typically rely on per-scene optimization over explicit 3D representations or cascaded edit-and-reconstruct pipelines, resulting in high test-time cost, limited 3D awareness, and structural inconsistencies. To couple appearance synthesis and geometry prediction during editing, we build on a unified RGB-geometry reconstruction-generation latent space and adapt it to feed-forward 3D scene editing. The resulting framework, \textbf{JointEdit3D}, performs asymmetric latent inpainting by observing only a single edited RGB reference latent and generating the remaining RGB views and edited geometry latent under source-scene anchoring. JointEdit3D introduces a dedicated SceneAnchor Branch to inject source-scene structure without forcing direct copying, and adopts edit/background-aware losses to balance edited-region fidelity with unedited-content preservation. To address the lack of paired resources for standardized 3D scene editing evaluation, we introduce SceneEdit3D-15K, a dataset with 15K paired editing samples and renderer-provided 3D annotations, together with SceneEdit3D-Bench, a curated 100-sample benchmark. Experiments show that JointEdit3D improves edited-region quality and 3D structural completeness over prior baselines while maintaining competitive background preservation.

</details>


### [52] [Dual-Constrained Diffusion Image Compression for Operational Rate-Distortion-Perception Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13366)
*Sanxin Jiang,Jiro Katto,Heming Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 DCIC（双约束扩散图像压缩），在单一码流和固定码率下，通过失真约束与幂等性约束引导扩散解码，实现可连续调节的率-失真-感知（RDP）权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典率失真理论难以同时刻画重建保真度与感知真实感；现有神经图像压缩方法虽能接近率-感知最优，但缺少可实际显式实现完整 RDP 曲面的框架，核心困难在于解码端公共随机性的引入。

Method: DCIC 将学习型基础编解码器与扩散式解码器结合，在反向去噪过程中加入两个约束：失真约束限制重建结果相对基础 codec 输出的偏离，幂等性约束要求恢复图像重新编码后能恢复基础 codec 重建结果，作为分布感知约束的可处理替代。通过迭代优化和一致噪声注入实现无额外码率开销的公共随机性，并用双衰减因子 KD、KP 控制失真与感知权衡。

Result: 在 CelebA-HQ、CLIC2020、ImageNet-1K 上，跨 CNN、Transformer 与混合架构实验表明，DCIC_RDP 在感知型 codec 中取得更优 BD-PSNR，DCIC_RP 在 BD-FID 上可匹配专门面向感知质量的方法。

Conclusion: DCIC 提供了一种实用的完整 RDP 曲面导航机制，可从单一码流连续调节保真度与真实感，并在保持固定码率的同时兼顾失真性能和感知质量。

Abstract: The rate-distortion-perception (RDP) trade-off extends classical rate--distortion theory by imposing a distributional constraint on reconstructions, providing a unified framework for neural image compression that jointly governs fidelity and perceptual realism. While prior work achieves near-optimal rate--perception trade-offs, practical frameworks explicitly realizing the full RDP surface remain scarce, primarily due to the difficulty of introducing common randomness at the decoder. We propose DCIC (Dual-Constrained Diffusion Image Compression), which integrates a learned codec with a diffusion-based decoder governed by joint distortion and idempotence constraints. The distortion constraint bounds reconstruction fidelity relative to the base codec output; the idempotence constraint -- requiring that re-encoding the restored image recovers the base codec reconstruction -- serves as a tractable surrogate for the distributional perception requirement. Together, they steer the reverse denoising process via iterative optimization with consistent noise injection, realizing common randomness without additional rate overhead. At fixed rate, dual attenuation factors $(K_D, K_P)$ jointly navigate the Pareto frontier of the distortion-perception plane, enabling continuously adjustable fidelity-realism trade-offs from a single bitstream. DCIC$_{RD}$ ($K_P{=}0$) and DCIC$_{RP}$ ($K_D{=}0$) arise as boundary curves, with DCIC$_{RDP}$ ($K_D = K_P=1$) realizing the optimal interior operating point. Experiments on CelebA-HQ, CLIC2020, and ImageNet-1K across CNN, Transformer, and hybrid architectures confirm that DCIC$_{RDP}$ achieves superior BD-PSNR over all perceptual codecs, while DCIC$_{RP}$ matches dedicated perception-oriented methods in BD-FID, validating the practical value of full RDP surface navigation.

</details>


### [53] [MoVerse: Real-Time Video World Modeling with Panoramic Gaussian Scaffold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13376)
*Yang Zhou,Ziheng Wang,Yuqin Lu,Haofeng Liu,Jun Liang,Shengfeng He,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoVerse 从单张窄视场图像生成可实时交互漫游的视频世界模型，通过先补全全景、再构建持久 3D Gaussian 空间记忆、最后进行条件视频渲染，实现单图到可控高质量漫游视频的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单张窄视场图像只包含环境的一小部分信息，但交互式漫游需要完整的周围场景、稳定几何结构、可控相机运动以及时间一致的高保真观测。因此，如何从极少输入构建可导航、可持续渲染的世界是核心挑战。

Method: 方法将任务拆分为世界构建与观测渲染：首先使用拓扑感知扩散将输入图像扩展为重力对齐的 360° 全景图，补全缺失视野；随后通过全景几何感知残差预测将全景提升为持久的 3D Gaussian scaffold，形成密集且可直接渲染的空间记忆；最后使用 Gaussian 条件视频渲染器沿用户指定相机轨迹生成照片级视频。为支持实时交互，作者训练双向扩散教师模型用于高质量条件渲染，并将其蒸馏为因果自回归学生模型，以实现有界延迟的视频流式输出。

Result: 系统可在单张 NVIDIA RTX 4090 GPU 上以 8 FPS 支持实时场景漫游，展示了从单图生成交互式视频世界的可行性。

Conclusion: MoVerse 结合显式 3D 表示的可控性与长期一致性，以及生成式视频模型的感知质量，为单图世界创建和交互式视频输出提供了一条实用技术路径。

Abstract: We present MoVerse, a real-time video world model that creates an interactively navigable scene from a single narrow-field-of-view image. This setting is challenging because the input observes only a small fraction of the environment, while interactive roaming requires a complete surrounding world, persistent geometry, controllable camera motion, and temporally coherent high-fidelity observations. MoVerse addresses this problem by separating world construction from observation rendering. It first expands the input into a gravity-aligned 360$^\circ$ panorama with topology-aware diffusion, closing the missing field of view before 3D reasoning. It then lifts the panorama into a persistent 3D Gaussian scaffold using panoramic geometry-aware residual prediction, yielding a dense and directly renderable spatial memory. Finally, a Gaussian-conditioned video renderer translates scaffold renderings along user-specified camera trajectories into photorealistic video. To make this renderer practical for interaction, we train a bidirectional diffusion teacher for high-quality conditional rendering and distill it into a causal autoregressive student for bounded-latency streaming. This design combines the controllability and long-range consistency of explicit 3D representations with the perceptual quality of generative video models. MoVerse supports real-time scene roaming at 8~FPS on a single NVIDIA RTX~4090 GPU, demonstrating a practical path toward single-image world creation with interactive video output.

</details>


### [54] [SmartFont: Dynamic Condition Allocation for Few-Shot Font Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13382)
*Zian Yang,Zixin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SmartFont 是一种基于扩散模型的少样本字体生成框架，通过全局内容/风格建模与弱监督局部纠错专家协同，实现结构完整性与局部风格细节的更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本字体生成需要同时保证字形的全局结构完整和局部风格一致。现有方法要么偏重全局内容-风格建模，鲁棒但解耦不充分；要么偏重局部/部件建模，细节好但依赖局部先验与参考覆盖。因此，核心问题在于如何在生成过程中有效组织互补但存在偏差的全局与局部条件。

Method: 论文提出 SmartFont：采用扩散式生成框架，结合全局内容-风格生成分支与弱监督局部纠错专家。局部分支在弱部件监督下学习专家级局部概念和语义空间映射，实现语义-空间分配；同时引入去噪状态条件分配模块，在不同时间步和注入模块中自适应加权全局内容、全局风格与局部纠错特征。

Result: 大量实验表明，SmartFont 在全局结构与局部细节之间取得更优平衡，提升了生成字形质量，并改善了局部风格细节保真度。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，少样本字体生成的关键不只是学习更纯粹的条件表示，而是根据生成阶段与空间语义动态分配全局和局部条件；SmartFont 通过多层次条件分配有效提升了字体生成质量。

Abstract: Few-shot font generation simultaneously requires global structural completeness and fine-grained local style fidelity. Existing methods usually either rely on global content-style modeling, which is robust but imperfectly disentangled, or emphasize component/local modeling, which captures fine details but relies heavily on local priors and reference coverage. We argue that the key challenge is not merely to learn purer conditions, but to organize complementary yet biased global and local conditions through multi-level allocation during generation. To this end, we propose SmartFont, a diffusion-based few-shot font generation framework that combines global content-style generation with weakly supervised local corrective experts. The local branch performs semantic-spatial allocation by learning expert-wise local concepts and semantically meaningful spatial maps under weak component supervision, enabling fine-grained correction without requiring explicit component-conditioned inference. On top of this, a denoising-state condition allocation module adaptively weights global content, global style, and local corrective feature across timesteps and injection blocks. Extensive experiments show that SmartFont achieves better global-local balance, improves glyph quality and local detail fidelity.

</details>


### [55] [Person Identification from Contextual Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13410)
*Igor Kviatkovsky,Ehud Rivlin,Ilan Shimshoni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究基于人体运动风格的身份识别，提出生成式概率模型，并首次将该任务扩展到交互式识别场景，通过自适应选择视觉提示来提升识别效率与准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于运动模式的身份识别多为被动观察，适用于监控或认证场景，但缺少主动交互机制。作者希望通过系统与被试的序列化交互，让系统选择最有区分度的刺激，从而更快、更可靠地确定身份。

Method: 论文构建动作实例生成过程的概率模型，并推导两类常见身份识别场景下的概率推断方案。进一步提出交互式框架：系统每轮向被试展示视觉提示，记录其动作响应；基于受 Human Information Processing 范式启发的生成模型预测响应，并选择能最大化期望响应与身份之间互信息的提示；随后用观测响应更新身份后验概率，直到达到置信度阈值。

Result: 作者在五个公开数据集以及一个自建数据集上进行实验。自建数据集包含22名被试对15种提示的4,476条响应记录。实验报告了较高的识别率，表明该方法在多数据集上具有有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作将运动风格身份识别从被动识别推进到主动交互式识别，利用互信息驱动的提示选择和贝叶斯后验更新实现高置信度分类，是该问题在交互式设定下的早期/首次系统化尝试。

Abstract: We consider the problem of identifying people based on their motion styles. We present a generative model describing the action instance creation process and derive a probabilistic identity inference scheme for two common person identification scenarios motivated by the surveillance and authentication applications. We introduce a novel, \emph{interactive}, scenario for person identification from motion patterns. To this end, we formalize the identification process in the context of a sequential message exchange session between the subject and the system. The subject's behavior is modeled using a probabilistic generative model inspired by the Human Information Processing (HIP) paradigm. At each stage, the system presents a visual stimulus (a cue) to the subject and records their motion response. The cue is selected so as to maximize the mutual information of the expected response and the subject's identity. Once recorded, the response is used to update the a posteriori probability over possible subjects' identities. The process terminates once a sufficient classification confidence level is reached. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first time person identification is addressed in such interactive setting. We report high recognition rates on five publicly available datasets and our own novel dataset consisting of 4,476 recordings of 22 test subjects responding to 15 cues.

</details>


### [56] [VietFashion: Benchmarking Sketch-Text Composed Image Retrieval for Cultural Outfits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13427)
*Hoang-Nguyen Cao,Le-Hoang Bui,Dinh-Khoi Vo,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VietFashion，一个围绕越南传统服饰 Ao Dai 的草图-文本组合图像检索基准，用于评估细粒度文化服饰检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化服饰的识别依赖细微结构与象征语义，标准视觉检索模型难以充分捕捉；设计场景中还需要同时利用手绘草图表达结构、文本描述表达文化语义。

Method: 构建包含 650 张初始草图的数据集，并利用生成模型扩展到 21,000 余张带对齐文本说明的逼真图像；文本属性提示从时尚杂志中提取以增强真实性与多样性；采用单个查询对应多个有效结果的多目标检索设定，并建立标准化评测协议，对现有组合图像检索方法进行基准测试。

Result: 实验显示，现有先进方法在细粒度文化语义建模和多模态组合理解方面仍存在显著性能差距。

Conclusion: VietFashion 为细粒度文化服饰检索提供了具有挑战性的公开基准，有助于推动草图-文本组合检索、文化语义理解和时尚设计检索研究。

Abstract: Cultural garments pose a unique challenge for visual retrieval systems, as their identity often depends on subtle structural and symbolic details that are poorly captured by standard AI models. We introduce VietFashion, a new benchmark for sketch-text composed image retrieval centered on the Ao Dai, a traditional Vietnamese garment. VietFashion enables designers and researchers to retrieve culturally meaningful outfits using a combination of hand-drawn sketches, which convey garment structure, and textual descriptions, which encode cultural semantics. The dataset is initialized with 650 sketches and expanded using generative models to produce over 21,000 photorealistic images with aligned captions. Textual prompts that describe detailed outfit attributes, which are extracted from fashion magazines to ensure authenticity and diversity. To better reflect the inherent ambiguity of design intent, VietFashion adopts a multi-target retrieval setting, where a single query may correspond to multiple valid results. We establish standardized evaluation protocols and benchmark state-of-the-art composed image retrieval methods. Experimental results reveal significant performance gaps in modeling fine-grained cultural semantics and multi-modal composition, positioning VietFashion as a challenging benchmark for fine-grained fashion retrieval. The dataset is publicly available at: https://hng0303.github.io/VietFashion.

</details>


### [57] [OmniDirector: General Multi-Shot Camera Cloning without Cross-Paired Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13432)
*Jiwen Liu,Shujuan Li,Zhixue Fang,Xiaohan Li,Yan Zhou,Zijie Meng,Zhimin Zhang,Yawen Luo,Guoxin Zhang,Yu-Shen Liu,Pengfei Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 OmniDirector，通过将相机参数编码为“相机网格运动视频”，并在百万级相机网格-视频对上训练，实现复杂、多镜头视频生成中的相机运动克隆与导演级可控生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机运动克隆方法存在两类主要问题：参数化表示难以支持多镜头生成；依赖合成交叉配对数据的方法受数据稀缺限制，导致复杂相机运动克隆效果不佳。因此需要一种通用、可扩展且适合多镜头控制的相机运动表示与训练框架。

Method: 作者提出一种通用相机运动表示，将相机编码为网格运动视频，以视觉化方式表达相机参数并支持多轨迹整合；在此基础上构建 OmniDirector 统一框架，使用百万级相机网格-视频配对数据训练，以协调角色、动作和相机控制；同时设计层次化提示扩展代理，通过理解控制信号关系，系统描述相机运动与视觉内容，实现多控制信号融合。

Result: 大量实验表明，该框架在相机运动克隆、复杂镜头控制和整体可控性方面优于现有方法，并表现出较强的多模态扩散 Transformer 控制能力。

Conclusion: OmniDirector 通过相机网格运动视频表示和大规模配对训练，为视频生成中的相机运动克隆提供了更通用、更精确的解决方案，尤其适用于复杂和多镜头场景。

Abstract: Cloning camera motion from reference videos is an important task in video generation, as videos provide intuitive and precise control. Existing methods either directly use parametric representations that fail to handle multi-shot generation or synthesize cross-paired data, which suffer from data scarcity, resulting in poor performance in complicated camera motion cloning. To address these issues, we introduce a general camera motion representation that encodes cameras as grid motion videos. This camera grid represents the camera parameters visually and supports the integration of diverse trajectories for multi-shot video generation. Building upon this, we propose OmniDirector, a unified framework trained on a million-scale camera grid-video pairs that coordinates characters, actions, and cameras to provide director-level control for multimodal diffusion transformers. Furthermore, we design a novel hierarchical prompt expansion agent that harmoniously integrates different control signals by systematically describing camera motion and visual content through understanding signal relationships. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior performance and outstanding controllability of our framework. Project page: https://ymlinfeng.github.io/OmniDirector.github.io/

</details>


### [58] [VISA: VLM-Guided Instance Semantic Auditing for 3D Occupancy World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13460)
*Ruiqi Xian,Yuehan Xian,Jing Liang,Xuewei Qi,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 VISA：一种用于3D语义占用世界模型的训练期语义审计方法，利用离线VLM对物体实例进行可靠性加权审计并蒸馏到语义logits中，在不改变推理流程的情况下提升闭集占用性能，尤其改善物体类和稀有类mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将3D体素或物体特征与图像裁剪字幕嵌入对齐的VLM策略，虽然能提升文本空间相似度，但并不能稳定提升闭集语义占用mIoU；同时物体与稀有类别错误会影响自由空间理解、碰撞检测和时序状态传播。因此需要一种更适合闭集占用任务的VLM使用方式。

Method: VISA在训练阶段对每个物理物体实例选择代表性图像裁剪，调用离线VLM生成结构化审计信息，包括类别假设、可能混淆、可靠性、属性和证据；随后沿物体轨迹传播该审计结果，并将其与匹配的3D物体体素对齐。训练中通过可靠性加权的分类体系损失、属性因子损失和场景级审计图损失，将审计信息蒸馏到语义logits中；推理阶段保持原模型不变且无需VLM。

Result: 在nuScenes上，三次运行平均结果显示，VISA将OccWorld的mIoU从19.06提升到20.05，将GaussianWorld的mIoU从21.36提升到21.91。在GaussianWorld上，物体mIoU从18.18提升到19.16，稀有类mIoU从15.60提升到16.79。

Conclusion: 研究表明，对于闭集3D语义占用任务，VLM更适合作为带可靠性建模的训练期语义审计器，而不是简单作为通用字幕嵌入对齐目标。VISA能在不增加推理成本的前提下提升语义占用质量，尤其对物体和稀有类别更有效。

Abstract: Semantic 3D occupancy provides a voxelized world state for autonomous driving and robot decision making, but object and rare-class errors can affect free-space interpretation, collision checking, and temporal state propagation. We show that a common VLM strategy, aligning 3D voxel or object features with crop-caption embeddings, improves text-space similarity without reliably improving closed-set occupancy mIoU. Motivated by this mismatch, we propose VISA, a training-time semantic auditing approach for existing occupancy world models. VISA queries an offline VLM on a representative crop of each physical object instance, obtains a structured audit with class hypotheses, plausible confusions, reliability, attributes, and evidence, and propagates it along the object track. The audit is grounded to matched 3D object voxels and distilled into semantic logits through reliability-weighted taxonomy, attribute-factor, and scene-level audit graph losses, while inference remains unchanged and requires no VLM. On nuScenes, averaged across three runs, VISA improves OccWorld from 19.06 to 20.05 mIoU and GaussianWorld from 21.36 to 21.91 mIoU; on GaussianWorld, object mIoU improves from 18.18 to 19.16 and rare-class mIoU from 15.60 to 16.79. These results suggest that VLMs are better suited to closed-set occupancy as reliability-aware semantic auditors than as generic caption-embedding targets.

</details>


### [59] [Point-Wise Geometry-Aware Transformer for Partial-to-Full Point Cloud Registration in Computer-Assisted Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13488)
*Siyu Zhou,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GAPR-Net用于部分到完整点云配准，通过卷积与Transformer结合、跨注意力融合局部与全局信息，在多种骨骼数据上取得高召回率和低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分到完整点云配准受重叠比例变化、点密度波动和噪声影响较大；现有Transformer方法多偏向全局上下文建模，忽略对精确对应关系至关重要的细粒度局部几何信息。

Method: 构建粗到细的学习式点云配准框架GAPR-Net，结合卷积模块与Transformer模块；利用跨注意力在部分点云和完整点云之间融合局部与全局特征；设计变换不变的逐点几何特征表示，以稳健刻画点与邻域之间的相对几何关系。

Result: 在胫骨、股骨、骨盆和胸椎软骨四类几何差异明显的骨骼数据上验证，整体配准召回率达到94.2%，RMSE为1.992 mm，旋转和平移的R²分别为0.908和0.974。

Conclusion: GAPR-Net能够有效解决部分到完整点云配准问题，在部分观测条件下实现高精度三维点云配准，可为计算机辅助手术中的精准导航和机器人介入提供基础。

Abstract: Partial-to-full registration remains challenging due to varying overlap ratios, fluctuating point densities, and the presence of noise. While transformers have shown strong potential for point cloud processing, prior methods typically confine them to global context aggregation, overlooking fine-grained local geometry crucial for accurate correspondence. We propose \emph{GAPR-Net}, a learning-based point cloud registration framework with a coarse-to-fine architecture that combines convolution and transformer modules, in which local and global information is fused between the partial and full point clouds using a cross-attention mechanism. To achieve this, a transformation-invariant point-wise geometric feature representation is proposed, which can robustly capture relative geometric features for individual points with respect to their neighboring points. To evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed approach, experiments are conducted on four geometrically distinct bones, including the tibia, femur, pelvis, and thoracic cartilage. The overall registration recall reaches 94.2\%, the method results in a low RMSE of 1.992 mm and $R^2$ values of 0.908 and 0.974 for rotation and translation, respectively. The results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively addresses the partial-to-full point cloud registration problem. The proposed method enables highly accurate 3D point cloud registration using partial observation, providing a critical foundation for precise surgical navigation and robotic interventions in computer-assisted surgery. The code will be accessed after the double-blind review process.

</details>


### [60] [Budget-Constrained Step-Level Diffusion Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13496)
*Mingkun Lei,Tong Zhao,Liangyu Yuan,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BudCache通过预先固定推理计算预算，并离线搜索最优步骤级缓存策略，在相同预算下相比启发式缓存方法获得更好的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型步骤级缓存方法通常依赖逐步误差阈值启发式规则来决定是否复用缓存，未直接针对最终输出质量进行优化，导致不同输入的推理延迟不稳定，部署时难以精确控制计算成本。

Method: 论文提出BudCache：先给定固定计算预算，再搜索最能保持最终生成质量的缓存策略。为降低步骤选择的组合搜索复杂度，方法结合模拟退火与确定性爬山进行离线策略搜索；推理阶段不需要在线搜索或阈值判断。对于极低计算预算场景，还提出缓存感知的时间步调度对齐，通过调整时间离散化以匹配所选缓存策略，减少缓存导致的生成轨迹偏移。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev和Wan2.1上的实验表明，在相同推理预算下，BudCache相比基于启发式阈值的缓存基线能够取得更好的生成质量，同时保持固定且可控的计算开销。

Conclusion: BudCache将步骤级缓存从阈值驱动的动态决策转化为预算约束下的离线策略优化，使扩散模型推理具备更稳定的延迟控制和更优的质量-效率权衡，尤其适合部署中需要固定算力预算的场景。

Abstract: Step-level caching accelerates diffusion models by exploiting temporal redundancy across denoising steps. Existing methods make per-step cache decisions using threshold-based heuristics, without directly optimizing for final output quality. As a result, their inference latency varies across inputs and is difficult to control at deployment. In this work, we propose BudCache, which inverts this formulation: rather than letting per-step error thresholds dictate the runtime cost, we fix the compute budget in advance and search for the cache policy that best preserves the final output. To tackle the combinatorial complexity of step selection, we combine Simulated Annealing with deterministic Hill Climbing. This offline search identifies high-quality cache policies within minutes and introduces no online search or thresholding overhead during inference. When the compute budget is very tight, we further introduce cache-aware schedule alignment, which adapts the time discretization to the selected cache policy to reduce cache-induced trajectory mismatch. Experiments on FLUX.1-dev and Wan2.1 show that BudCache achieves better generation quality than heuristic caching baselines under the same inference budgets. Code is available at https://github.com/Westlake-AGI-Lab/BudCache

</details>


### [61] [Heterogeneous LiDAR Early Fusion and Learned Re-Ranking Strategy for Robust Long-Term Place Recognition in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13503)
*Judith Vilella-Cantos,Juan José Cabrera,Mónica Ballesta,David Valiente,Luis Payá*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MinkUNeXt-VINE++，通过异构 LiDAR 早期融合与推理阶段学习式重排序，提升葡萄园等非结构化、重复场景中的地点识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业田地、葡萄园等非结构化环境对自主系统定位提出挑战；LiDAR 不受光照影响且能提供三维信息，但在重复性强的场景中，单传感器地点识别容易混淆，难以找到真正匹配位置。

Method: 方法结合 Livox Mid-360 与 Velodyne VLP-16 两种异构 LiDAR 数据进行早期融合，以获得更完整的环境表征；同时在推理阶段引入学习式 re-ranking 策略，对候选匹配结果重新排序，以增强重复环境中的真阳性检索能力。

Result: 在 TEMPO-VINE 葡萄园数据集上进行评估，覆盖不同物候阶段。结果显示，相比单传感器方法和现有先进方法，MinkUNeXt-VINE++ 显著提升地点识别性能；Recall@1 相比单传感器方法提升约 20%，结合重排序后提升约 30%。

Conclusion: 异构 LiDAR 早期融合与学习式重排序能够有效增强非结构化、重复农业环境中的 LiDAR 地点识别鲁棒性，MinkUNeXt-VINE++ 在葡萄园定位任务中表现出较强应用潜力。

Abstract: Robust localization in unstructured environments, such as agricultural fields, is a critical challenge for autonomous systems. LiDAR sensors provide detailed 3D information about the environment and are invariant to lighting conditions. For this reason, LiDAR-based place recognition methods have gained significant attention. In this paper, we propose MinkUNeXt-VINE++, a novel approach that combines early fusion of heterogeneous LiDAR data from two sensors (Livox Mid-360 and Velodyne VLP-16) and a learned re-ranking strategy in inference time. This fusion leverages the strengths of each sensor to provide a more comprehensive representation of the environment. Additionally, the re-ranking approach is particularly important in repetitive environments, such as vineyards, as finding true positives is a major challenge. We evaluated our approach using the TEMPO-VINE dataset, which provides heterogeneous LiDAR data in vineyard environments across different phenological stages. Our results demonstrate that MinkUNeXt-VINE++ significantly improves place recognition performance compared to single-sensor approaches and state-of-the-art methods. MinkUNeXt-VINE++ achieves a 20% improvement in the Recall@1 metric compared to single-sensor approaches, and +30% including re-ranking. The code of our method is publicly available for reproduction.

</details>


### [62] [Measurement-Calibrated Multi-Camera Fusion for Vision-Based Indoor Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13509)
*Mateo Toro Diz,Jonathan Hoss,Noah Klarmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究室内视觉定位中如何利用单摄像头误差显式表征来校准并优化多摄像头数据融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内视觉定位受检测噪声、遮挡和摄像头覆盖范围限制影响，定位流程多个环节存在不确定性。现有多摄像头融合通常被视为黑箱，并主要通过端到端结果评估，难以解释其具体贡献机制。

Method: 提出一种测量校准的数据融合方法，对单摄像头定位误差进行组件级量化，分别分析单应性标定、人体检测和运动跟踪带来的误差，并将这些误差信息用于多摄像头融合的校准与优化。

Result: 实验表明，多摄像头融合相比单摄像头基线提升了定位精度。测量校准融合相较标准融合在绝对精度上的提升有限，但显著降低了轨迹方差，并改善了运动平滑性。

Conclusion: 显式误差表征有助于理解和设计视觉室内定位系统中的数据融合策略，尤其适用于需要稳定、连续运动估计的应用场景。

Abstract: Indoor vision-based localization systems are affected by detection noise, occlusions, and limited camera coverage, leading to uncertainty at multiple stages of the pipeline. While multi-camera data fusion is widely used to mitigate these issues, it is typically treated as a black-box component and evaluated solely end-to-end, obscuring its mechanistic contributions. To address this gap, this work investigates whether explicitly characterizing single-camera localization errors can be leveraged to calibrate and optimize multi-camera data fusion.
  We introduce a measurement-calibrated fusion approach that integrates component-wise error quantification, specifically isolating homography calibration, human detection, and motion tracking. A component-wise evaluation is conducted to quantify error contributions from homography calibration, human detection, and motion tracking.
  Experimental results show that data fusion improves localization accuracy compared to single-camera baselines. While measurement-calibrated fusion provides only limited improvement in absolute accuracy over standard fusion, it substantially reduces trajectory variance and improves motion smoothness, which are critical for applications requiring stable and continuous motion estimates. These results highlight the value of explicit error characterization when designing data fusion strategies for vision-based indoor positioning systems.

</details>


### [63] [MaskWAM: Unifying Mask Prompting and Prediction for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13515)
*Hanyang Yu,Haitao Lin,Jingbo Zhang,Wenyao Zhang,Chenghao Gu,Heng Li,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MaskWAM 是一种面向机器人控制的对象中心世界-动作模型，通过将对象掩码同时作为输入与预测目标，缓解文本指令歧义和 RGB 预测缺乏语义约束的问题，从而提升策略泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频预测的 World Action Models 在复杂场景中存在空间表达瓶颈：文本输入容易产生指代歧义，尤其在多物体或杂乱环境中难以精确定位目标；同时，非结构化 RGB 未来帧预测缺少对象级语义 grounding，容易受到任务无关背景和视觉噪声影响。论文旨在引入更明确的空间锚点和对象级监督，以提升机器人在语言明确与语言模糊任务中的泛化与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 MaskWAM，一个对象中心的 world-action model。其核心做法是将对象掩码同时纳入模型输入与预测输出，并通过统一的 Mixture of Transformers（MoT）框架联合建模。模型利用未来掩码预测提供对象中心的语义监督，以抑制背景噪声；同时结合首帧视觉提示，例如目标物体掩码，为任务目标提供精确空间锚点，降低自然语言描述中的指代歧义。

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboTwin 以及真实机器人任务上的评估表明，MaskWAM 相比基线方法在语言明确任务和语言模糊任务中均取得显著更优表现。摘要尤其强调，直接使用掩码条件比仅依赖文本条件能提供更强的视觉引导，对未见物体操作具有更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MaskWAM 证明了在视觉驱动的机器人世界-动作模型中，引入对象掩码作为显式输入与预测监督是一种有效范式。该方法通过对象级语义监督和空间锚定机制，缓解文本歧义与背景干扰问题，从而增强机器人控制策略的泛化能力和真实场景适应性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) present a promising paradigm for robotic control via video prediction. However, current WAMs suffer from fundamental spatial bottlenecks: standard text inputs introduce referential ambiguity in cluttered scenes, while unstructured RGB predictions lack semantic grounding and remain biased by task-irrelevant backgrounds. To overcome these limitations, we introduce MaskWAM, an object-centric world-action model. By jointly integrating masks as both explicit inputs and predictions via a unified Mixture of Transformers (MoT), MaskWAM unlocks robust policy generalization. This design provides two key benefits: (1) predicting future masks yields object-centric semantic supervision that suppresses visual noise, significantly enhancing even standard text-conditioned WAMs; and (2) coupling this predictive supervision with first-frame visual prompts, such as target object masks, establishes a precise spatial anchor that substantially reduces language ambiguity. Crucially, as WAMs are inherently vision-driven architectures, direct mask conditioning yields substantially stronger guidance than text alone, establishing a precise and robust paradigm for manipulating unseen objects. Evaluations on LIBERO, RoboTwin, and real-world tasks demonstrate that MaskWAM significantly outperforms baselines in both language-clear and language-ambiguous tasks.

</details>


### [64] [What's Old is New Again: Classical Dimensionality Reduction for Efficient Saliency-Guided Biometric Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13528)
*Samuel Webster,Walter Scheirer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于PCA/LDA等经典降维思想的自动显著性图生成方法，用于生物特征呈现攻击检测中的显著性引导训练，在无需人工标注和领域知识的情况下提升模型鲁棒性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有显著性获取方法常依赖人工标注、领域专家知识或特定数据集，成本高、可扩展性差、迁移到新PAD场景困难，限制了显著性引导训练在生物特征攻击检测中的广泛应用。

Method: 作者从原始训练数据直接生成受PCA和LDA启发的显著性图，并将其用于显著性引导训练；在已有显著性研究的虹膜PAD、合成人脸检测、指纹PAD中验证效果，并扩展到指静脉PAD和身份证PAD等新领域。

Result: 在所有测试领域中，使用降维来源显著性图训练的模型均超过普通基线，并在部分情况下优于现有SOTA显著性方法，同时不需要额外人工标注、领域工具或资源投入。

Conclusion: 基于经典降维的显著性获取为PAD任务提供了一种低成本、高可扩展、跨领域适用的方案，缓解了显著性引导训练落地中的关键瓶颈，并可能推广到更广泛的视觉识别任务。

Abstract: Saliency-guided training is a paradigm in visual recognition that encourages models to focus on the most relevant image regions during learning. While its application in biometric presentation attack detection (PAD) has shown strong benefits in robustness and generalization, adoption is often limited by the high cost, domain specificity, and limited scalability of existing saliency acquisition methods, such as human annotations over a limited dataset. We present a novel, cost-efficient, and highly-scalable approach to saliency acquisition using maps inspired by classical dimensionality reduction techniques: PCA and LDA. Our proposed methods generate saliency maps directly from raw training data, requiring no human annotation nor domain knowledge. We contextualize the effectiveness of these saliency sources in three saliency-explored domains (iris PAD, synthetic face detection, fingerprint PAD) and demonstrate its scalability in two saliency-novel domains (fingerprint vein PAD and ID card PAD). Across all domains tested, models trained using dimensionality reduction-sourced saliency maps exceed baseline and sometimes SOTA saliency methods without any resource investment or domain-specific tooling. Our findings overcome an important yet unaddressed barrier to saliency-guided training for biometric attack detection and beyond.

</details>


### [65] [Edit the Bits, Diff the Codes: Bitwise Residual Editing for Visual Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13558)
*Shengqiang Zhang,Ruotong Liao,Volker Tresp,Barbara Plank,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BitResEdit 是一种面向位级残差 VAR 图像生成器的免训练文本引导图像编辑方法，通过位级决策引导与多尺度残差代码组合，实现更强的局部文本对齐并保持背景稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VAR 图像编辑方法多在 token 流、特征或普通 next-token logits 上操作，未充分利用位级残差 VAR 模型的两个原生结构：逐 bit Bernoulli 预测头，以及用于组装图像的加性多尺度残差代码场。因此，作者希望更精细地控制“采样什么”和“改写到图像代码的哪里”。

Method: 提出 BitResEdit，由 BitEdit 和 ResEdit 组成。BitEdit 在 post-CFG 的逐 bit log-odds 上进行 source-negative guidance，利用共享编辑前缀计算源-目标对比方向，并将更新投影到围绕干净 CFG 采样器的闭式 Bernoulli-KL 信任域中。ResEdit 将采样 bit 转换为各尺度连续代码残差，使用局部化 mask 进行门控，再通过生成器原生的多尺度残差求和机制重新注入。

Result: 在 PIE-Bench 和 Infinity-2B 上，BitResEdit 在同骨干 VAR 编辑器中取得最强的文本对齐表现；编辑区域 CLIP 分数相比最强先前方法提升 +1.07，同时背景保持能力仍具有竞争力。消融实验显示 BitEdit 和 ResEdit 分别在目标对齐与背景保持方面互补。

Conclusion: BitResEdit 证明了直接利用位级 Bernoulli 决策结构和多尺度残差代码组合机制，可以在无需训练的情况下提升 VAR 图像编辑的局部可控性、文本一致性和背景保真度。

Abstract: Text-guided image editing with visual autoregressive (VAR) generators requires controlling both what the model samples and where the sampled change is written back into the image code. Existing VAR editors mainly operate on token streams, features, or flat next-token logits, leaving two native structures of bitwise-residual VAR models underused: the per-bit Bernoulli prediction head and the additive multi-scale residual code field from which the image is assembled. We propose BitResEdit, a training-free editor for bitwise-residual VAR generators such as Infinity. BitEdit performs source-negative guidance by tilting the post-CFG per-bit log-odds along a source--target contrast computed on a shared edited prefix, then projects each update into a closed-form Bernoulli-KL trust region around the clean CFG sampler. ResEdit converts the sampled bits into per-scale continuous-code residuals, gates them with a localization mask, and re-injects them through the generator's native sum-of-scales. Together they couple decision-time bit guidance with combination-time code composition, so masked-out latent features are preserved exactly by code arithmetic while localized, scale-aware edits are applied inside the target region. On PIE-Bench with Infinity-2B, BitResEdit attains the strongest text alignment among same-backbone VAR editors, improving CLIP on the edited region by +1.07 over the strongest prior editor while keeping background preservation competitive with it. Ablations show BitEdit and ResEdit play complementary roles in target alignment and background preservation.

</details>


### [66] [Contrast-Informed Augmentation and Domain-Adversarial Training for Adult-to-Neonatal MR Reconstruction Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13562)
*Stephen Moore,Lara Leijser,Richard Frayne,Roberto Souza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究如何提升基于深度学习的MRI重建模型从成人脑MRI到新生儿脑MRI的跨域泛化能力。结果表明，对成人数据进行新生儿对比度启发的数据增强，并结合域对抗训练，可显著改善新生儿欠采样MRI重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成人MRI数据通常更易获得，而新生儿MRI数据有限且与成人数据存在明显域偏移，包括组织对比度、解剖结构和成像特征差异。直接用成人数据训练的E2E-VarNet在新生儿数据上可能泛化不足，因此需要探索增强和域适配方法来提高其跨域鲁棒性。

Method: 研究比较三种训练策略：仅使用未增强成人数据训练；使用未增强成人数据与新生儿对比度启发增强成人数据混合训练；在混合训练基础上加入域对抗目标。模型在回顾性欠采样的多线圈成人T2加权脑MRI上训练，并在成人和新生儿测试集上以R=4和R=8加速因子评估。评价包括SSIM、PSNR等定量指标、定性视觉评估，以及通过t-SNE分析潜在特征表示。

Result: 在新生儿测试数据上，混合训练和混合域对抗训练均优于仅成人未增强训练。R=4时，Mixed-DAT表现最佳，SSIM为0.924±0.027，PSNR为33.98±1.15 dB。R=8时，Mixed-DAT在SSIM上最佳，为0.848±0.031，高于Unaug-Only的0.766±0.037和Mixed的0.814±0.035；PSNR方面Mixed略优，为29.56±0.83 dB，Mixed-DAT为29.43±0.83 dB。t-SNE结果显示，Mixed-DAT增加了成人未增强、成人增强和新生儿样本潜在表示之间的重叠。

Conclusion: 对比度感知数据增强与域对抗训练能够改善E2E-VarNet从成人到新生儿MRI重建任务的泛化性能，说明该策略有助于缓解欠采样新生儿MRI重建中的域偏移问题，并提高模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: Purpose: To investigate whether contrast-informed data augmentation and domain-adversarial training improve the adult-to-neonatal generalization of the E2E-VarNet. Methods: Three training regimes were investigated: (1) adult-only training with unaugmented adult data, (2) mixed training with paired unaugmented and neonatal-informed augmented adult data, and (3) mixed training with a domain-adversarial objective. Models were trained on retrospectively undersampled multi-coil adult T2-weighted brain MR data and evaluated on neonatal and adult test data at acceleration factors $R=4$ and $R=8$ using quantitative metrics and qualitative evaluation. Feature analyses assessed whether domain-adversarial training altered the latent representations of unaugmented adult, augmented adult, and neonatal test samples. Results: Mixed training (Mixed) and mixed domain-adversarial training (Mixed-DAT) outperformed unaugmented adult-only training (Unaug-Only) when evaluated on neonatal data. At R=4, Mixed-DAT achieved the best performance (SSIM = 0.924 +/- 0.027, PSNR = 33.98 +/- 1.15 dB). At R=8, Mixed-DAT performed best when measured using SSIM (0.848 +/- 0.031 vs. 0.766 +/- 0.037 for Unaug-Only and 0.814 +/- 0.035 for Mixed) and Mixed performed best when measured using PSNR (29.56 +/- 0.83 dB vs. 26.26 +/- 0.78 dB for Unaug-Only and 29.43 +/- 0.83 dB for Mixed-DAT). Qualitative assessment of t-SNE plots suggested that Mixed-DAT increased the overlap among the latent representations of the unaugmented adult, augmented adult, and neonatal test data. Conclusion: Contrast-informed augmentation and domain-adversarial training improved adult-to-neonatal generalization of deep learning-based MR reconstruction. These findings suggest that contrast-informed data augmentation combined with adversarial training may improve robustness to domain shift in undersampled neonatal MR reconstruction.

</details>


### [67] [EvTexture++: Event-Driven Texture Enhancement for Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13580)
*Dachun Kai,Jiayao Lu,Yueyi Zhang,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 EvTexture++，一个面向视频超分辨率的事件驱动纹理增强框架，利用事件相机的高频时空信息提升纹理恢复，并作为即插即用模块增强现有 VSR 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件辅助视频超分辨率方法多将事件信号用于运动估计和时序对齐，而较少专门挖掘事件中蕴含的高频细节来恢复纹理；同时，大运动场景下纹理区域容易出现跨帧不一致和闪烁问题。

Method: 方法包含定制化纹理增强分支和迭代纹理增强模块，逐步利用高时间分辨率事件信息恢复纹理；此外引入时序纹理对齐模块，通过事件引导的纹理感知光流实现跨帧纹理精确对齐，并支持作为即插即用组件集成到现有 VSR 模型。

Result: 在五个数据集上的实验表明 EvTexture++ 达到当前最优性能；集成到近期 VSR 模型后显著提升效果，在纹理丰富的 Vid4 数据集上 PSNR 最高提升 1.55 dB。

Conclusion: EvTexture++ 证明事件信号不仅可用于运动建模，也能有效服务于纹理增强和时序一致性维护，为事件辅助视频超分辨率提供了新的研究方向和实用增强模块。

Abstract: Event-based vision has drawn increasing attention owing to its distinctive properties, including ultra-high temporal resolution and extreme dynamic range. Recent works have introduced it to video super-resolution (VSR) to enhance flow estimation and temporal alignment. In contrast, this paper shifts the focus of event signals from motion refinement to texture enhancement in VSR. We propose EvTexture++, the first event-driven framework dedicated to texture enhancement in VSR. It leverages high-frequency spatiotemporal details from events to improve texture recovery. EvTexture++ incorporates a customized texture enhancement branch, along with an iterative texture enhancement module that progressively exploits high-temporal-resolution event information for texture restoration. This enables gradual refinement of texture regions across iterations, yielding more accurate and detailed high-resolution outputs. Besides intra-frame texture recovery, large motions could degrade inter-frame temporal consistency, particularly in texture regions, leading to texture flickering. To mitigate this, we further exploit the continuous-time motion cues of events to enhance temporal consistency, introducing a temporal texture alignment module that estimates event-guided texture-aware flow for precise inter-frame texture alignment. Moreover, EvTexture++ is designed as a plug-and-play tool to flexibly boost the performance of existing VSR models. Experiments on five datasets demonstrate that EvTexture++ achieves state-of-the-art performance. When integrated into recent VSR models, it yields significant improvements, with gains of up to 1.55 dB in PSNR on the texture-rich Vid4 dataset. Code: https://github.com/DachunKai/EvTexture.

</details>


### [68] [Towards Effective Waste Segmentation for Automated Waste Recycling in Cluttered Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13587)
*Mamoona Javaid,Mubashir Noman,Abdul Hannan,Shah Nawaz,Mustansar Fiaz,Sajid Ghuffar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种面向自动废弃物回收的高效废弃物分割网络，结合空间域局部结构建模与频谱域全局上下文建模，并通过辅助特征增强模块改善复杂场景下的边界与目标区域分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市扩张和人口增长导致废弃物急剧增加，亟需高效自动化管理。现有深度学习废弃物分割方法虽效果较好，但通常依赖大型骨干网络，计算效率不足，且在杂乱场景中性能下降。

Method: 设计级联式废弃物分割网络：在空间域捕获局部结构依赖，在频谱域提取全局上下文关系，逐步融合互补域的局部与全局表征；同时引入辅助特征增强模块（AFEM），强化目标边界并放大目标块状区域，以提升复杂背景下的分割质量。

Result: 在 ZeroWaste-aug、ZeroWaste-f 和 SpectralWaste 数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法相较相关方法具有更好的废弃物分割表现，尤其在杂乱场景中体现出优势。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，空间域与频谱域的互补建模以及针对边界和目标区域的特征增强，能够在保持网络效率的同时提升自动废弃物分割的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Rapid expansion of urban areas and population growth is causing an immense increase in waste production, which demands the need for efficient and automated waste management. In this scenario, automated waste recycling (AWR) using deep learning methods can assist humans in optimal waste management. Recent deep learning approaches for AWR provide promising waste segmentation performance, however, these methods rely on large backbone networks that are inefficient for AWR systems and suffer from performance deterioration in cluttered scenes. To this end, an optimal waste segmentation network is introduced which effectively utilizes the spatial domain to capture localized structural dependencies and the spectral domain to efficiently extract global contextual relationships. This cascaded design allows the network to progressively leverage both local and global representations across complementary domains to highlight the semantic information necessary for effective segmentation of various waste objects. Furthermore, auxiliary feature enhancement module (AFEM) is introduced to enhance the target objects' boundaries and blob amplification for better segmentation in cluttered scenarios. Extensive experimentation on ZeroWaste-aug, ZeroWaste-f and SpectralWaste datasets reveals the merits of the proposed method.

</details>


### [69] [Revisiting Vehicle Color Recognition in Long-Tailed Surveillance Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13625)
*Vinícius Orrú,Bruno H. Foggiatto,Gabriel E. Lima,David Menotti,Rayson Laroca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究严重类别不平衡场景下的车辆颜色识别，结合生成式少数类数据增强与多种训练/预处理策略，在真实监控数据集上显著提升宏平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆颜色是监控系统中车辆识别的重要线索，尤其在车牌因低分辨率、遮挡、运动模糊或光照差而不可用时更关键。但真实场景中车辆颜色类别分布高度不平衡，单纯总体准确率难以反映稀有颜色的识别能力。

Method: 作者基于UFPR-VeSV真实监控数据集，研究严重类别不平衡下的车辆颜色识别；采用两类现成生成策略进行少数类合成增强：RunDiffusion/JuggernautXL的文本条件图像生成，以及Gemini 2.0 Flash的图像条件颜色编辑。随后将筛选后的合成数据与现代视觉表征、损失重加权、学习率调度、颜色安全增强、前景感知预处理和集成融合结合。

Result: 最佳方法达到94.6%的micro accuracy和79.7%的macro accuracy，相比近期文献将macro accuracy提高了8.2个百分点。人工错误分析显示，许多剩余错误对人类标注者也具有视觉歧义。

Conclusion: 合成少数类数据增强结合面向类别不平衡的训练策略能有效提升车辆颜色识别，尤其改善稀有类别表现；但在无约束监控图像中，颜色识别存在由视觉模糊和标注歧义带来的实际性能上限。

Abstract: Vehicle color recognition is an important cue for vehicle identification in surveillance systems, especially when license plates are illegible due to low resolution, occlusion, motion blur, or poor illumination. However, real-world vehicle color distributions are highly imbalanced, making overall accuracy insufficient to assess performance on rare but operationally relevant colors. This paper presents a comprehensive study of vehicle color recognition under severe class imbalance using UFPR-VeSV, a challenging real-world surveillance dataset. We investigate synthetic minority-class augmentation through two off-the-shelf generative strategies: text-conditioned image generation with RunDiffusion/JuggernautXL and image-conditioned color editing with Gemini 2.0 Flash. The curated synthetic data are combined with modern visual representations, loss reweighting, learning-rate scheduling, color-safe augmentation, foreground-aware preprocessing, and ensemble fusion. The bestperforming approach achieves 94.6% micro accuracy and 79.7% macro accuracy, improving macro accuracy by 8.2 percentage points over recent literature. A manual error analysis further shows that many remaining failures are visually ambiguous even for human annotators, highlighting the practical limits of color-based vehicle identification in unconstrained surveillance imagery. The generated images and source code are publicly available at https://github.com/viniciusorru/vcr-synthetic

</details>


### [70] [Surflo: Consistent 3D Surface Flow Model with Global State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13644)
*Antoine Guédon,Shu Nakamura,Nicolas Dufour,Jiahui Lei,Ko Nishino,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Surflo 将可变数量的未标定 RGB 视图压缩为一个由 K 个潜在 token 表示的全局 3D 状态，并通过 flow matching 从噪声生成任意数量的有向 3D 表面点，实现全局一致且任意分辨率的前馈重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何结构对视角不变，多视图图像本质上是同一 3D 状态的冗余编码。现有前馈重建方法未充分利用这一点：逐视图方法会产生重叠且未对齐的点图，输出规模随输入视图数线性增长；全局潜变量方法则受限于固定且低分辨率的输出表示。
