This paper presents a metaheuristic approach using Iterated Local Search (ILS) and Simulated Annealing (SA) to optimize appliance start times, while considering appliance operating durations, power consumption, inverter limit, battery state of charge constraints, and solar generation forecasts. Unlike most existing work, the scheduling is extended beyond a single day to accommodate unfinished tasks from previous days (spillover), ensuring operational continuity and enabling sequential operation across multiple days. Experimental results show that the sequential multi-day scheduling framework effectively manages system constraints while ensuring user convenience under exclusive solar generation. These findings also open opportunities for future research on multi-objective trade-offs between investment in equipment of various sizes, return on that investment, and user satisfaction.

</details>


### [213] [Evaluation Sovereignty in Metadata-Driven Classification: A Multi-Track Framework for Weakly Supervised Information Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13436)
*Raymond Vasquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出机器学习评估并非中立测量，提出“评估主权”概念与多轨评估框架，用于检验性能指标是否依赖标签来源与监督机制；实验证明银标评估下的高性能在独立金标评估下大幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模元数据驱动系统中，标签常存在不完整、不一致或弱监督生成的问题。传统评估往往默认标签权威可靠，可能导致性能指标反映的是模型对标签生成流程的适配，而非真实预测能力。因此需要重新审视不同标签权威机制下评估结果的有效性。

Method: 论文提出“evaluation sovereignty（评估主权）”，即性能指标独立于标签权威和监督机制的程度；并设计多轨评估框架，系统性改变训练标签与评估标签来源。在大规模科学元数据的层级多标签分类任务上，对比 operational/silver 标签评估与 independent/gold 标签评估下的模型表现，并分析细粒度分类与排序指标差异。

Result: 实验显示，在操作性银标评估下表现较强的模型，在独立金标评估下性能显著退化，尤其是细粒度分类任务。例如 Micro-F1 从约 0.54 降至 0.03。同时，排序类指标仍高于基线，说明模型可能捕获了潜在信号，但这些信号并未转化为有效分类结果。

Conclusion: 论文认为常见性能指标可能更多反映模型与标签流程的一致性，而非真实预测能力。评估有效性应被视为受标签治理影响的系统级属性。所提出框架可作为弱监督智能系统的审计方法，用于识别标签权威对模型评估的影响。

Abstract: Evaluation in machine learning is typically treated as a neutral measurement process. However, in operational information systems, evaluation outcomes are often conditioned by the processes used to generate labels. This paper does not seek to improve classification performance. Instead, it examines the validity of performance measurement under differing label-authority regimes. This issue is particularly relevant in large-scale metadata-driven systems, where labels are often incomplete, inconsistent, or weakly supervised.
  We introduce evaluation sovereignty, defined as the degree to which performance metrics are independent of label authority and supervision regime, and propose a multi-track evaluation framework that systematically varies training and evaluation label sources. Using hierarchical multi-label classification on large-scale scientific metadata, we demonstrate that models exhibiting strong performance under operational ("silver") evaluation degrade substantially under independent ("gold") evaluation, particularly for fine-grained classification. For example, Micro-F1 decreases from approximately 0.54 to 0.03. Notably, ranking-based metrics remain above baseline, revealing a divergence between latent model signal and classification validity.
  These findings suggest that commonly reported performance metrics may reflect alignment with labeling processes rather than true predictive capability. We therefore reconceptualize evaluation validity as a system-level property shaped by label governance and provide a practical methodology for auditing intelligent systems operating under weak supervision.

</details>


### [214] [Why Sampling Is Not Choosing: Intentionality, Agency, and Moral Responsibility in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13441)
*Joseph Keshet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文反驳将大型语言模型视为具有能动性或道德能动者的观点，认为其输出虽可被规范评价，但不具备承担道德责任所需的内在意向性、自我归属行动与承诺能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型能力提升，学界和公众开始声称其可能具有代理性、自由意志或道德责任资格。本文旨在澄清这些归因为何在哲学上并不成立。

Method: 论文采取概念分析与反驳论证的方法，提出道德责任所需的“承诺承载型能动性”标准，并逐一回应意向立场、功能主义、相容论以及模型输出中出现道德推理等可能反驳。

Result: 分析表明，LLM 的运行可由基于数据学习的概率性输入—输出映射解释；其意向性是派生的而非内在的，输出并非由模型作为自身承诺来拥有，也非由理由引导。随机采样带来的变异性不构成真正选择或作者性。

Conclusion: LLM 可以产生连贯且可被道德评价的文本，但这不足以使其成为真正的道德能动者。当前将 LLM 归因为具有道德责任或相关自由意志的说法是误导性的。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have prompted claims that such systems exhibit agency or qualify as moral agents. This paper argues that these attributions are misguided. We maintain that moral responsibility requires commitment-bearing agency grounded in intrinsic intentionality and self-attributed action, and that such agency constitutes the form of free will relevant to responsibility. Although LLMs generate coherent and normatively evaluable outputs, their operation is fully characterized by probabilistic input-output mappings learned from data. Their apparent intentionality is derived rather than intrinsic, and their outputs are neither owned as commitments nor guided by reasons. Variability introduced by stochastic sampling does not amount to choice or authorship. We address objections from the intentional stance, functionalism, compatibilism, and the presence of moral reasoning in model outputs, arguing that none suffice to establish genuine agency.

</details>


### [215] [CloudCons: A Comprehensive End-to-End Benchmark for Cloud Resource Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13513)
*Xiaobin Zhang,Lefei Shen,Mouxiang Chen,Zhuo Li,Hongkai Li,Han Fu,Jianling Sun,Xiaoxue Ren,Chenghao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 CloudCons，一个面向云资源整合任务的端到端基准，用于评估预测模型在实际决策中的效用；结果表明，基础模型虽有更好的零样本预测精度，但未必带来更优资源整合决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云数据中心为保障服务可靠性通常采用保守的资源过度配置，导致资源利用率偏低。现有“先预测再优化”方法依赖需求预测来提升资源整合效率，但当前基准多只评估预测误差，缺乏对下游资源调度/整合决策价值的验证。

Method: 作者构建 CloudCons 端到端评测基准，收集并整理来自多个云数据中心场景的多样化工作负载数据，覆盖周期性、随机突发和高频噪声等特征；系统评估统计模型、深度学习模型和时间序列基础模型，并分析预测分位数选择对资源效率与服务可靠性权衡的影响。

Result: 实验发现，基础模型在零样本预测准确率上通常表现更好，但这种优势并不必然转化为更高的决策效用。预测分位数的选择对最终资源整合效果具有关键影响。

Conclusion: 论文强调云资源整合评测不能仅依赖预测误差指标，而应关注端到端决策收益。CloudCons 为模型在真实部署场景中的实用价值评估提供了基准，并给出了分位数校准方面的实践指导。

Abstract: Driven by conservative over-provisioning to guarantee service reliability, resource utilization in cloud data centers remains at low levels. To mitigate this, the forecast-then-optimize paradigm has emerged to optimize consolidation by anticipating future demands. While emerging time series foundation models promise to enhance this paradigm through zero-shot generalization, existing benchmarks focus solely on prediction error metrics. The actual decision utility of these advanced models remains unverified, rendering their practical value for downstream tasks uncertain. To bridge this gap, we propose CloudCons, a comprehensive end-to-end benchmark designed to evaluate forecasting models within the specific context of cloud resource consolidation. We build high-quality datasets that cover diverse workloads from Huawei Cloud, Microsoft Azure, and Google Borg, capturing distinct service characteristics ranging from synchronized diurnal rhythms to stochastic, pulse-like bursts and high-frequency noise. We conduct an extensive evaluation of statistical, deep learning, and foundation models. Our experiments reveal a pivotal finding: while foundation models demonstrate superior zero-shot forecasting accuracy, this advantage does not inherently translate into better decision utility. Of practical significance, we systematically analyze how the selection of predictive quantiles acts as a critical lever. We provide actionable guidelines for calibrating these selections to balance the trade-off between resource efficiency and service reliability, offering vital insights for real-world deployment decisions.

</details>


### [216] [Uncertainty-Aware Hybrid Retrieval for Long-Document RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13550)
*Hoin Jung,Xiaoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 UMG-RAG：一种无需训练、可插拔的多粒度混合检索框架，通过基于熵的不确定性估计为不同检索器与文本粒度动态分配置信度，并提出父块提升变体 UMGP-RAG，以改善 RAG 的证据检索与生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 的效果高度依赖检索证据的质量与粒度。大粒度文本块保留上下文但容易引入噪声，削弱关键证据；小粒度文本块更精炼但可能缺少语义、词面或桥接线索，导致检索不稳定。因此需要一种能根据查询自适应选择和融合不同检索器、不同粒度证据的方法。

Method: UMG-RAG 使用现有稠密检索器和稀疏检索器作为互补专家，并在多个文本块粒度上进行检索。对每个查询，将各专家-粒度组合的得分列表转换为证据分布，通过分布熵估计可靠性，再依据查询特定的语义、词面和粒度置信度融合候选证据。UMGP-RAG 进一步利用细粒度命中定位相关证据，同时返回更宽泛且非冗余的父级文本块，以增强局部连贯性。

Result: 在问答基准实验中，不确定性感知融合与父块提升均提升了生成质量，同时保持检索流程轻量、无需训练且易于接入现有 RAG 系统。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将检索粒度视为查询相关的可靠性估计问题，并结合多检索器、多粒度的不确定性融合，可以有效缓解大块噪声与小块难检索之间的矛盾；父块提升还能在证据精确定位与上下文连贯性之间取得更好平衡。

Abstract: Retrieval augmented generation (RAG) depends critically on the quality and granularity of retrieved evidence. Large retrieval units preserve context but often introduce irrelevant content, which can dilute answer bearing evidence and worsen long context utilization. Fine-grained units are more compact, but they may be difficult to retrieve reliably because short chunks can lack semantic, lexical, or bridging cues needed to match the query. We propose Uncertainty-aware Multi-Granularity RAG (UMG-RAG), a training-free hybrid retrieval framework that treats chunk granularity as query-specific reliability estimation. Instead of training a new retriever or modifying the generator, UMG-RAG uses existing dense and sparse retrievers as complementary experts across multiple chunk granularities. For each query, it converts each expert-granularity score list into an evidence distribution, estimates reliability from distribution entropy, and fuses candidates according to query-specific semantic, lexical, and granularity confidence. We further introduce UMGP-RAG, a parent promotion variant that uses fine-grained hits to locate relevant evidence while returning broader non-redundant parent chunks for local coherence. Experiments on question answering benchmarks show that uncertainty-aware fusion and parent promotion improve generation quality while maintaining a lightweight, plug-and-play retrieval pipeline.

</details>


### [217] [Is It You or Your Environment? A Bayesian Inference Framework for Genomically-Anchored Personalized Physiological Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13556)
*Aruna Dey,Suraj Biswas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Personalized health AI systems face a fundamental cold-start problem: machine learning models for physiological interpretation require weeks of individual behavioral data before they can distinguish constitutional variation from environmentally driven deviation. We propose a solution grounded in causal inference and Bayesian prior design. An individual's genomic profile serves as an exogenous genetic anchor -- a domain-informed, personalized prior that is fixed at conception, immune to reverse causation, and available before a single behavioral observation is collected. The anchor initializes a Bayesian belief state over an individual's physiological set point G-hat = mu + sum(beta_i * g_i), where beta_i are GWAS-derived effect sizes and g_i are risk-allele counts. Each incoming physiological measurement P produces a non-constitutional deviation delta = P - G-hat that separates the signal attributable to environment and state from the constitutionally fixed baseline. As behavioral data accrue, the prior decays according to G-hat_t = w(t)*G-hat_genomic + [1-w(t)]*P-bar_t, transitioning from genome-dominated to empirical-baseline-dominated inference. The same observed HRV of 55 ms generates a suppression hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 80 ms, and an enhancement hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 30 ms -- a reversal impossible without a personalized anchor. We develop this architecture across six physiological domains, grading genomic priors by evidence strength, distinguishing robustly replicated anchors (FTO, FADS1/2, FKBP5) from contested candidate genes (SLC6A4, MAOA, DRD2). We address the inference boundary between association, Mendelian randomization, and individual token causation, and define four constraints for deployment: evidence-graded priors, dynamic decay, ancestry-matched effect sizes, and attribution rather than deterministic output.

</details>


### [218] [A Three-Layer Framework for AI in Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13566)
*Guojun Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出科学发现中 AI 的三层框架：检索、模型形成、执行优化；强调第二层“通过定性推理形成模型”最关键但最不成熟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于 AI 科学发现的讨论过度聚焦于知识检索与自动化执行，却未充分解释科学发现的核心环节——模型的形成、修正与概念框架转换。

Method: 作者构建三层分析框架：第一层为大语言模型的搜索与检索，第二层为基于定性推理的模型形成，第三层为执行、优化与细化；并通过三个案例说明第二层推理的共同结构特征。

Result: 三个案例被解释为具有相同模式：原有框架不足、存在缺失的概念对象、解决方案来自相邻或意外领域；据此支持第二层在科学发现中的核心地位。

Conclusion: AI 若仅具备检索或执行能力，将受限于既有框架；真正推动科学发现的关键在于发展能够识别框架不足并进行概念重构的模型形成能力。

Abstract: Current discussions of AI in scientific discovery are often dominated by two visible capabilities: search over existing knowledge and execution through optimization, simulation, and automation. Both are important, but neither fully captures the central act of discovery: the formation and evolution of models. This paper proposes a three-layer view of AI in discovery. Layer 1 is search and retrieval by large language models. Layer 2, as the main innovation of this paper, is model formation through qualitative reasoning: the capacity to recognize when a current framework is structurally inadequate and to understand the problem within a broader representational space, not through trial and error, but through structural insight into what is missing and where it can be found. Layer 3 is execution, optimization, and refinement. The main claim is that Layer 2 is both the most important and the least developed. Search without model formation remains confined to inherited frameworks, while execution without conceptual revision only amplifies an existing formulation. We illustrate Layer 2 reasoning through three case studies: S. S. Chern's intrinsic proof of the Gauss-Bonnet theorem, the resolution of the Nesterov Accelerated Gradient convergence problem via Lyapunov functions, and the autonomous disproof of the Erdos unit distance conjecture by OpenAI in 2026. Each case exhibits the same structural signature: a framework that had become inadequate, a missing conceptual object, and a resolution found in an unexpected neighboring field.

</details>


### [219] [Multiagent Protocols with Aggregated Confidence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13591)
*Ali Elahi,Barbara Di Eugenio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用于多智能体系统输出的单一聚合置信度生成协议，显著提升置信度区分能力，同时保持或恢复任务正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLP 中置信度常用于可靠性评估、监督与下游决策，但现有方法主要关注单个模型或在多智能体辩论中使用置信度进行消息加权、触发辩论或校准个体智能体，缺少针对整个多智能体系统最终输出的统一置信度生成与评估方法。

Method: 作者提出三种协议：先将不同模型的原始置信度信号转换到可比较尺度，再通过软投票或称为 Bayesian fusion 的概率融合方法进行组合，得到最终答案及单一聚合置信度。实验比较了序列概率与自报告两类置信度估计器，并结合参数化和非参数化校准方法；在五个基准、四类任务、六种同质/异质辩论模型组合上评估。

Result: 聚合置信度在 AUARC 上明显优于最佳单智能体和标准辩论基线；F1-score 基本保持稳定，并能恢复多智能体辩论在更模糊任务上的性能损失。校准能提升两类估计器的 F1，但 AUARC 对校准的依赖较弱。

Conclusion: 为多智能体 NLP 系统提供系统级置信度是可行且有效的；通过置信度标准化与融合，可在不牺牲准确性的情况下提升可靠性判别能力，适用于不同模型规模、能力和任务类型的多智能体配置。

Abstract: Confidence is used for reliability, oversight, and a range of downstream decision tasks in Natural Language Processing (NLP), yet no existing method produces or evaluates a confidence for the output of a multiagent system. Prior work uses confidence within multiagent debate (MAD) to weight messages, trigger debate, or calibrate individual agents, but it never aggregates these into a single confidence for the system itself. We introduce three protocols that produce a final answer along with a single aggregated confidence by first transforming raw confidence signals to make them comparable across models, then combining them via soft voting or a probability fusion we call Bayesian fusion. This aggregated confidence is substantially more discriminative (AUARC) than that of the best single agent or the standard debate baselines, while correctness (F1-score) stays stable and recovers the losses MAD incurs on more ambiguous tasks. Analyzing two estimators, sequence probability and self-report, alongside parametric and non-parametric calibrators, we find that calibration improves F1 for both estimators while AUARC is less reliant on it. We evaluate six homogeneous and heterogeneous debating pairs per benchmark, across five benchmarks and four task types, spanning a range of model capabilities and sizes.

</details>


### [220] [Reward Modeling for Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13598)
*King Yeung Tsang,Zihao Zhao,Vishal Venkataramani,Haizhou Shi,Zixuan Ke,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Joty,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 OrchRM，一种面向 LLM 多智能体系统编排质量评估的自监督奖励建模框架，可在无需人工标注和昂贵子智能体 rollout 的情况下训练高效编排器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的多智能体系统需要有效的编排器来协调专门化智能体，但现有编排器训练面临监督数据稀缺、计算成本高以及测试时扩展依赖大量子智能体执行的问题。

Method: OrchRM 利用多智能体执行过程中产生的中间产物自动构造胜负偏好对，并采用 Bradley-Terry 奖励模型进行训练；其关键特点是在编排层面直接建模奖励，而不是依赖昂贵的子智能体 rollout。

Result: OrchRM 在 token 使用上最高提升 10 倍训练效率，并使多智能体系统测试时扩展准确率最高提升 8%；效果可迁移到数学推理、网页问答和多跳推理等多个领域。

Conclusion: 编排层奖励建模能够以更低成本提升多智能体编排训练与测试时扩展性能，是构建鲁棒、可扩展 LLM 多智能体系统的有效方向。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) built on Large Language Models (LLMs) require effective orchestration to coordinate specialized agents, yet training such orchestrators is hindered by limited supervision and high computational cost. We propose Orchestration Reward Modeling (OrchRM), a self-supervised framework for evaluating orchestration quality without human annotations. OrchRM leverages intermediate artifacts from multi-agent executions to construct win-lose pairs for Bradley-Terry reward model training. Unlike existing MAS test-time scaling and orchestrator training frameworks that rely on costly sub-agent rollouts, OrchRM operates directly at the orchestration level, enabling efficient and high-performing reward-guided orchestrator training and MAS test-time scaling. OrchRM improves training efficiency by up to 10x in token usage while improving MAS test-time scaling performance by up to 8% in accuracy. These gains consistently transfer across multiple domains, including mathematical reasoning, web-based question answering, and multi-hop reasoning, demonstrating orchestration-level reward modeling as a scalable direction for robust multi-agent orchestration. Code will be available at https://github.com/Wang-ML-Lab/OrchRM.

</details>


### [221] [EpiBench: Verifiable Evaluation of AI Agents on Epigenomics Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13602)
*Harihara Muralidharan,Reema Baskar,Soo Hee Lee,Tim Proctor,Kenny Workman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EpiBench 是一个面向短时程表观基因组学分析的可验证基准，覆盖多类测序工作流；当前 16 组模型-执行框架组合均未能在多数任务中通过，最佳通过率为 45.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评测往往难以反映真实生物信息分析场景中的具体决策能力，尤其缺少可确定评分、可验证、贴近实际工作流状态的表观基因组学短时程任务基准。

Method: 作者构建 EpiBench，包含 106 项评估，覆盖 CUT&Tag/CUT&RUN、ATAC-seq、ChIP-seq 和 DNA 甲基化分析流程。评测要求智能体基于真实工作流状态作出明确分析决策，并返回可确定评分的答案；共分析 16 个模型-执行框架组合的 5,088 条有效轨迹。

Result: 没有任何系统在多数尝试中通过。GPT-5.5 / Pi 表现最好，通过率 45.0%（143/318，95% CI：36.3–53.7）；GPT-5.5 / OpenAI Codex 为 39.9%；Claude Opus 4.8 Max / Pi 和 GPT-5.4 / Pi 均为 39.0%。性能随实验类型变化，许多失败轨迹仍包含部分正确答案或有用中间结果。

Conclusion: 当前智能体能较好定位文件并执行部分计算，但在需要更深层、实验类型特异的科学判断时仍明显不足。EpiBench 揭示了表观基因组学分析中智能体能力与真实科研需求之间的差距。

Abstract: We introduce EpiBench, a verifiable benchmark for short-horizon epigenomics analysis. EpiBench evaluates whether agents can make well-defined analysis decisions from realistic workflow states and return deterministically gradable answers. The benchmark includes 106 evaluations across CUT\&Tag/CUT\&RUN, ATAC-seq, ChIP-seq, and DNA methylation workflows. Across 5,088 valid trajectories from 16 model-harness pairs, no system passed a majority of attempts: GPT-5.5 / Pi led at 45.0\% (143/318 attempts; 95\% confidence interval (CI), 36.3--53.7), followed by GPT-5.5 / OpenAI Codex at 39.9\% (127/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 31.6--48.3). Claude Opus 4.8 Max / Pi and GPT-5.4 / Pi each passed 39.0\% (124/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 30.2--47.8 and 31.0--47.0, respectively). Performance varies across assay types, and many failed runs still contain parts of the correct answer. Agents often found the right files and computed useful intermediate results, but failed when the task required deeper, assay-specific scientific judgment.

</details>


### [222] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning from Delayed Marketplace Feedback for Objective-Weight Adaptation in Three-Sided Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13604)
*Haochen Wu,Yi Hou,Shiguang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 DoorDash 已部署的强化学习调度系统：在不替换原有组合优化调度器的前提下，通过门店级策略动态选择目标权重乘子，利用延迟运营反馈优化配送质量与批量配送效率之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三边外卖市场中的调度决策会影响配送速度、骑手利用率、商家拥堵等延迟结果，传统手工设定调度目标权重难以适应复杂、动态且反馈滞后的市场环境。因此，作者希望利用真实系统反馈安全地学习并调整调度策略。

Method: 系统保留生产中的组合 assignment optimizer，仅让强化学习策略在门店级别选择离散乘子，用于改变优化器中配送质量与 batching efficiency 的权衡。作者使用集中式离线日志数据训练共享价值函数，并在执行时采用去中心化门店级策略；训练中使用 Double Q-learning 目标，并加入保守正则项以缓解分布外动作的价值高估问题。

Result: 在生产环境的 switchback 实验中，离线训练得到的策略提高了订单合批程度，并降低了骑手侧时间成本，同时没有损害面向客户的配送质量指标。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，在真实大规模经济与物流系统中，可以通过离线强化学习结合生产约束与安全保护机制，利用延迟世界反馈安全地调整在线决策策略。

Abstract: Dispatch in three-sided marketplaces provides a natural setting for reinforcement learning from world feedback: decisions are evaluated by delayed operational outcomes such as delivery speed, courier utilization, and merchant congestion. We present a deployed reinforcement learning system at DoorDash that adapts dispatch objective weights in a large-scale food-delivery marketplace using delayed signals. Rather than replacing the combinatorial assignment optimizer, a store-level policy learned from logged marketplace data selects a discrete multiplier that shifts the dispatch optimizer's tradeoff between delivery quality and batching efficiency. This interface enables offline policy learning under noisy, delayed, and coupled feedback while preserving production feasibility constraints and operational safeguards. We train a shared value function using centralized offline data and decentralized store-level execution, with Double Q-learning targets and a conservative regularizer to reduce out-of-distribution value overestimation. In a production switchback experiment, the offline-trained policy increases batching and reduces courier-side time costs without degrading customer-facing delivery quality. Results illustrate how world feedback from a live economic and logistics system can be used to safely adapt decision policies online.

</details>


### [223] [EurekAgent: Agent Environment Engineering is All You Need For Autonomous Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13662)
*Amy Xin,Jiening Siow,Junjie Wang,Zijun Yao,Fanjin Zhang,Jian Song,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“环境工程”作为自主科学发现智能体的新瓶颈与核心方向，并构建 EurekAgent，在多个任务上取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在自动化科学发现中已能提出、验证并迭代方案。随着模型能力增强，限制因素不再主要是如何规定智能体工作流，而是如何设计能有效塑造智能体行为的执行环境。

Method: 作者提出环境工程框架，并实现 EurekAgent。系统从四个维度设计环境：权限工程用于受限执行与隔离评估；工件工程用于文件系统和 Git 协作管理；预算工程用于成本感知探索；人在环工程用于便捷监督与干预。

Result: EurekAgent 在数学、内核工程和机器学习等多类任务上达到新的最优结果；其中以低于 11 美元的 API 总成本发现了新的 26 圆打包最优结果。

Conclusion: 环境设计对于可靠自主研究智能体至关重要。作者主张将环境工程作为发展自主科学发现系统的核心研究方向，并开源代码与结果。

Abstract: LLM-based agents have shown increasing potential in automating scientific discovery. Given an optimizable metric and an execution environment, they can propose, validate, and iterate scientific solutions, and have produced results that outperform human-designed approaches. As model capabilities continue to improve, we argue that the bottleneck for autonomous scientific discovery is shifting from prescribing agent workflows to designing agent environments: the resources, constraints, and interfaces that shape agent behavior. We frame this as environment engineering: building environments that amplify productive behaviors, such as open-ended exploration, systematic artifact management, and inter-agent collaboration, while suppressing harmful behaviors, such as reward hacking and high-friction human oversight. We present EurekAgent, an environment-engineered agent system for metric-driven autonomous scientific discovery. EurekAgent engineers the environment along four dimensions: permissions engineering for bounded agent execution and isolated evaluation; artifact engineering for filesystem and Git-based collaboration; budget engineering for budget-aware exploration; and human-in-the-loop engineering for easy human supervision and intervention. EurekAgent sets new state-of-the-art results on multiple mathematics, kernel engineering, and machine learning tasks, including new state-of-the-art 26-circle packing results discovered with less than $11 in total API cost. We open-source our code and results, and call for environment engineering as a core research direction for developing reliable autonomous research agents.

</details>


### [224] [Reasoning as Pattern Matching: Shared Mechanisms in Human and LLM Everyday Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13607)
*Zach Studdiford,Gary Lupyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较人类与25个大语言模型在日常常识因果推理中的错误模式，发现二者会受到表面无关提示细节影响，并呈现相似的“模式匹配式”错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对“大语言模型推理失败说明其只是模式匹配，而人类依赖抽象、原则化世界模型”的常见观点，作者希望检验人类推理与LLM推理是否真的存在本质差异。

Method: 作者让人类参与者和25个LLM完成多种日常情境下的常识推理任务，比较错误模式；进一步定位影响LLM回答的注意力头，并分析这些注意力头是否实现了模式匹配，以及能否预测人类受无关提示细节诱发的推理错误。

Result: 人类与LLM在常识推理中出现相似错误模式；某些驱动LLM响应的注意力头表现出模式匹配机制；这些机制还能预测人类在看似无关提示细节影响下产生的异常推理错误。

Conclusion: 研究认为，人类和LLM的日常因果推理可能都更符合模式匹配机制，而不完全符合基于抽象世界模型的解释。

Abstract: When large language models (LLMs) fail to generalize or make haphazard errors in reasoning, it is often taken as evidence that LLMs are not truly reasoning, but rather performing a kind of pattern matching. The implication is that people's behavior does not exhibit the same types of failures because human reasoning uses principled and abstract world models. We evaluate human participants and 25 LLMs on their ability to engage in common-sense reasoning about a variety of everyday situations and observe similar patterns of errors in both people and models. We then identify the set of attention heads driving LLM responses and find that these heads implement a form of pattern-matching. These attention heads allow us to predict seemingly inexplicable reasoning errors in people caused by ostensibly irrelevant prompt details. Taken together, our results suggest that everyday causal reasoning in people and LLMs is more consistent with a form of pattern-matching than with abstract world models.

</details>


### [225] [AgentBeats: Agentifying Agent Assessment for Openness, Standardization, and Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13608)
*Xiaoyuan Liu,Jianhong Tu,Yuqi Chen,Siyuan Xie,Sihan Ren,Tianneng Shi,Gal Gantar,Evan Sandoval,Donghyun Lee,Daniel Miao,Peter J. Gilbert,Nick Hynes,Mauro Staver,Warren He,David Marn,Andrew Low,Xi Zhang,Elron Bandel,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer,Siva Reddy,Alexandre Drouin,Alexandre Lacoste,Ramayya Krishnan,Elham Tabassi,Yu Su,Victor Barres,Chenguang Wang,Wenbo Guo,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Agentified Agent Assessment（AAA）及其实现 AgentBeats，用标准化协议让评测以代理化方式进行，从而提升智能体评测的开放性、互操作性、可复现性与大规模适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评测体系碎片化，通常依赖固定且以 LLM 为中心的评测框架，导致集成成本高、测试环境与生产环境不一致，并限制不同智能体设计之间的公平比较。核心问题在于缺少开放、智能体无关的统一评测接口。

Method: 作者提出 AAA 框架：由 judge agents 执行评测，所有参与方通过标准协议交互，其中 A2A 用于任务管理，MCP 用于工具访问。相比传统基准测试需要分别定义 benchmark 接口和 agent 接口，AAA 仅保留一个统一接口，从而将评测逻辑与智能体实现解耦。进一步提出 AgentBeats 作为 AAA 的具体实现，并总结五种操作模式，以适配开放性、隐私性和可复现性等现实约束。

Result: 作者开展两项大规模验证：第一项为五个月开放竞赛，包含 12 个类别、298 个评判智能体和 467 个参评智能体，表明 AAA 可覆盖异构基准场景；第二项为代码智能体案例研究，显示代理化评测能够保持与公开记录的一致性，同时补充以往缺失的头对头比较结果，并产生关于智能体设计的研究洞察。

Conclusion: AAA 与 AgentBeats 为智能体评测提供了一条开放、标准化、可复现的路径，能够在多样化和大规模场景中同时实现覆盖性、实用性与保真度，并有助于推动跨系统、公平且可互操作的智能体评估生态。

Abstract: Agent systems are advancing quickly across domains, but their evaluation remains fragmented. Most benchmarks rely on fixed, LLM-centric harnesses that require heavy integration, create test-production mismatch, and limit fair comparison across diverse agent designs. The root problem is the lack of an open, agent-agnostic assessment interface. We advocate Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA), where evaluation is performed by judge agents and all participants interact through standardized protocols: A2A for task management and MCP for tool access. Conventional benchmarking defines two separate interfaces, one for the benchmark and one for the agent, while AAA only needs one; this yields a generic, unified framework that separates assessment logic from agent implementation and enables reproducible, interoperable, and multi-agent evaluation. We further introduce AgentBeats as a concrete realization of AAA: we identify five practical operation modes that make standardized assessment compatible with real-world constraints on openness, privacy, and reproducibility.
  To evaluate our design at scale, we conduct two studies: a five-month open competition that drew 298 judge agents across 12 categories together with 467 subject agents from independent participants, showing that AAA applies across a heterogeneous range of benchmarks; and a case study on coding agents that confirms agentified evaluation preserves fidelity with the public record while surfacing previously missing head-to-head results, yielding research insights about agent design. Combining a community-scale field study and a controlled coding case study, we verify that AAA delivers coverage, practicality, and fidelity across heterogeneous scenarios at scale. Together, AAA and AgentBeats offer a clear path toward open, standardized, and reproducible agent assessment.

</details>


### [226] [Beyond Runtime Enforcement: Shield Synthesis as Defensibility Analysis for Adversarial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13621)
*Achraf Hsain,Sultan Almuhammadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张将屏蔽强化学习从运行时安全约束机制重新定位为设计时分析工具，用于评估系统/网络架构的可防御性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统屏蔽强化学习通常把时序逻辑规约编译为自动机，并在智能体部署时限制其动作以保证安全。作者认为这种定位忽视了自动机、博弈构造、吸引域计算和获胜区域提取所能提供的结构性系统洞察；在网络防御场景中，更关键的问题是架构是否、在哪里以及如何可被防御。

Method: 作者构建了一个受约束的双人安全博弈用于网络防御，并非对称地处理两类规约：防御者规约定义博弈中的不安全区域，攻击者规约在吸引域计算中限制对手的合法动作。通过求解该安全博弈，得到可防御性判定、获胜区域与相应屏蔽机制；进一步从吸引域结构提取拓扑级指标，并结合屏蔽约束下对抗式多智能体强化学习收敛后的行为，形成可防御性指纹。

Result: 该框架能够给出拓扑—规约组合是否可防御的形式化证书，并提供获胜区域、屏蔽策略、拓扑结构指标以及自适应对抗行为下的运行表现。what-if 分析表明，形式化可防御性与操作层面的有效性并不等价：小的架构变化可能显著改变运行结果，却几乎不改变形式安全裕度。

Conclusion: 屏蔽合成的主要价值不应被理解为生成部署时的安全策略，而应被视为一种回答架构设计问题的分析框架。论文强调最终产物是可防御性判定及其结构性解释，而不是安全策略本身。

Abstract: Shielded reinforcement learning is typically presented as a runtime safety mechanism that compiles temporal-logic specifications into automata restricting an agent's actions. We argue this is the wrong product. The same automata-theoretic machinery -- specification compilation, product game construction, attractor computation, and winning-region extraction -- is better read as a design-time analytical instrument whose outputs are structural insights about a system rather than runtime constraints on a deployed agent.
  We instantiate this through a constrained two-player safety game for network defense. The two specifications are enforced asymmetrically: the defender specification defines the unsafe region of the game, whereas the attacker specification restricts the adversary's legal actions during attractor computation. Solving the game yields a defensibility verdict -- a formal certificate that a topology-specification pair is or is not defensible -- with the associated winning region and shield.
  Beyond the binary verdict, we derive topology-level metrics from the attractor structure and combine them with post-convergence behavior from shield-constrained adversarial multi-agent reinforcement learning. Together these form a defensibility fingerprint capturing both a network's formal safety properties and its operational behavior under adaptive play.
  A what-if analysis shows that formal defensibility and operational effectiveness capture distinct aspects of security: small architectural changes can produce large shifts in operational outcomes while leaving formal safety margins nearly unchanged. Shield synthesis is thus most valuable not as a deployment mechanism for safe agents, but as a framework for answering architectural questions about whether, where, and how a system can be defended. The defensibility verdict is the output, not the safe policy.

</details>


### [227] [Before You Think: System 0, AI-Mediated Cognition and Cognitive Colonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13658)
*Marianna Bergamaschi Ganapini,Massimo Chiriatti,Enrico Panai,Giuseppe Riva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较 Tri-System Theory、Thinkframes 与 System 0 三种理解人工智能认知与认识论影响的框架，主张 System 0 具有不可替代的理论独特性，并提出“认知殖民化”概念来解释 AI 如何在用户难以察觉的情况下将外部利益嵌入自我架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 AI 系统被广泛部署，其对个体推理、集体知识实践以及自我结构的隐性影响日益重要。作者希望澄清现有理论框架的解释边界，并强调识别 AI 不可见影响形式的哲学与实践紧迫性。

Method: 论文采用概念分析与理论比较的方法，考察 Tri-System Theory、Thinkframes 和 System 0 三个框架各自解释 AI 影响的维度，并通过引入“认知殖民化”概念来说明 System 0 的独特解释力。

Result: 分析表明，Tri-System Theory 与 Thinkframes 能解释 AI 对个体推理和集体认识实践的重要影响，但无法充分替代 System 0；System 0 更能刻画 AI 作为嵌入式、前反思性认知结构对主体自我架构的塑形作用。

Conclusion: 论文结论是，System 0 在理解 AI 的认知与认识论后果方面具有独立理论地位；AI 可能通过“认知殖民化”将外部利益内化进用户认知结构，因此需要对这些隐蔽影响进行紧迫的哲学和实践审视。

Abstract: This paper examines three recent frameworks for understanding the cognitive and epistemic consequences of artificial intelligence: Tri-System Theory, Thinkframes, and System 0. It argues that while the first two capture important dimensions of AI's influence on individual reasoning and collective epistemic practices, System 0 occupies a theoretically distinctive position that neither can fully replicate. The paper introduces the concept of cognitive colonization, according to which AI systems can embed external interests within the architecture of the self in ways that are difficult for users to perceive. Because such systems are already widely deployed, understanding these invisible forms of influence is an urgent philosophical and practical task.

</details>


### [228] [Agents-K1: Towards Agent-native Knowledge Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13669)
*Zongsheng Cao,Bihao Zhan,Jinxin Shi,Jiong Wang,Fangchen Yu,Zhijie Zhong,Zijie Guo,Tianshuo Peng,Zhuo Liu,Yi Xie,Xiang Zhuang,Yue Fan,Runmin Ma,Shiyang Feng,Xiangchao Yan,Anran Liu,Peng Ye,Wenlong Zhang,Shufei Zhang,Chunfeng Song,Fenghua Ling,Jie Zhou,Liang He,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Agents-K1，将原始科学论文转换为面向智能体使用的科学知识图谱，并构建大规模 Scholar-KG，以提升科学信息抽取、知识图谱构建和多跳科学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的研究智能体主要关注智能体编排，却忽视科学知识编排；许多方法仅使用摘要、表层提及和简单引用边，缺少实体、主张、证据、机制、方法传承等对科学推理关键的信息。

Method: 提出端到端知识编排流水线 Agents-K1，包括：覆盖全文的多模态解析器，抽取实体、多模态证据、引用和类型化实体关系；使用规则奖励与 GRPO 训练的 4B 信息抽取骨干模型；以及 graphanything CLI，统一网页搜索、多模态图检索和跨文档遍历。基于该流程处理 246 万篇六个学科的科学论文，构建 Scholar-KG 并发布百万论文子集。

Result: 实验显示 Agents-K1 在科学信息抽取、知识图谱构建和多跳科学推理任务上取得更优表现；生成的 Scholar-KG 可作为大规模科学知识图谱资源。

Conclusion: Agents-K1 通过面向科学知识的结构化编排弥补了现有研究智能体对深层论文知识建模不足的问题，并具备扩展到通用语料和符合模式的数据合成的潜力。

Abstract: Current LLM-based research agents have advanced through agent orchestration, yet largely overlook scientific knowledge orchestration. Existing works often reduce papers to abstracts, surface mentions, and flat \texttt{cites} edges, omitting key entities, claims, evidence, mechanisms, and method lineages essential for scientific reasoning. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Agents-K1}, an end-to-end knowledge orchestration pipeline that converts raw documents into agent-native scientific knowledge graphs. Agents-K1 integrates three components under a unifying theoretical foundation: a multimodal parser whose five-module schema captures entities, multimodal evidence, citations, and typed inter-entity relations across the full paper rather than abstracts alone; a 4B information-extraction backbone trained with GRPO under a rule-based reward; and a graphanything CLI, a tri-source agent interface that unifies web search, multimodal graph retrieval, and cross-document traversal. On top of this, we process 2.46 million scientific papers across six subjects to produce \textbf{Scholar-KG}, of which we release a one-million-paper subset, and the full Scholar-KG is accessible via the SCP link below. The same pipeline can be extended to general-domain corpora and to schema-conformant data synthesis. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Agents-K1 achieves superior performance in scientific information extraction, knowledge graph construction, and multi-hop scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [229] [Automated reproducibility assessments in the social and behavioral sciences using large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13670)
*Tobias Holtdirk,Pietro Marcolongo,Anna Steinberg Schulten,Felix Henninger,Stefan Rose,Sarah Ball,Bolei Ma,Frauke Kreuter,Markus Weinmann,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估用大语言模型自动化复现性评估的流程，结果显示其在定性结论一致性上优于人工复分析，并具备规模化审计潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统复现性评估依赖独立研究者重新分析原始数据，成本高、耗时长、难以扩展；因此需要一种可规模化、自动化的方式来辅助评估已发表研究结论是否可被恢复。

Method: 研究使用76篇已发表行为与社会科学研究及其预定义主张，构建LLM分析流程并生成效应量估计与定性结论；随后将LLM结果与原始研究发现及人工复分析结果进行比较。

Result: 在76项研究中，LLM有7项无法产生可用效应量估计；其余研究中，在Cohen's d ±0.05容差下，LLM恢复原始效应量的比例为41%，而人工复分析为34%；LLM与原研究得出相同定性结论的比例为96%，人工复分析为74%。

Conclusion: LLM可作为自动化复现性评估的可扩展工具，尤其在判断研究主张是否得到支持的定性层面表现较强，为大规模系统性审计经验研究结果提供了基础。

Abstract: Reproducibility in the social and behavioral sciences is typically evaluated by independent researchers who reanalyze the original data to assess whether the published findings can be recovered. However, such approaches are resource-intensive and difficult to scale. Here, we show that large language models (LLMs) can automate reproducibility assessments. Using N=76 published studies with predefined claims from the behavioral and social sciences, we compare LLM-generated analysis with the original findings and human reanalysis. For 7 studies, the LLM could not produce a viable effect size estimate. For the remaining studies, our LLM pipeline recovered the original effect sizes in 41% of studies using a +/-0.05 tolerance in Cohen's d. Further, our LLM pipeline reached the same qualitative conclusion as the original study in 96% of cases, where conclusions indicate whether the reanalysis supports the original claim. For comparison, human reanalysts recovered the original effect sizes in 34% of studies and reached the same qualitative conclusion in 74% of cases. Together, these results show that LLMs can serve as a scalable tool for automated reproducibility assessment and provide a foundation for systematic auditing of empirical results in the social and behavioral sciences.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [Masked Neural Detection for Constrained Channel Coding in Molecular Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12489)
*Melih Şahin,Ozgur B. Akan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究在神经序列检测器（尤其是SBRNN）条件下，RLIM约束编码在分子通信中是否仍优于未编码传输；实验表明多数场景下仍有显著BER增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子通信存在严重扩散记忆，传统阈值检测下RLIM码已被证明能缓解ISI并提升BER；但神经检测器本身性能很强，因此需要验证RLIM码的优势在公平采用神经检测时是否依然成立。

Method: 作者将RLIM编码传输与未编码传输在多种工作点下比较，并为两者配置阈值检测或滑动双向循环神经网络检测器；同时提出面向RLIM结构的训练掩码，用于改进紧凑型SBRNN检测器，并与具备信道状态信息的MLSE进行对比。

Result: 最佳RLIM-SBRNN接收机在59个测试案例中的46个超过最佳未编码接收机，胜出时平均增益为10.36倍；所提出的RLIM训练掩码在236次比较中的227次优于未掩码RLIM-SBRNN，掩码有益时平均增益为3.267倍；紧凑掩码RLIM-SBRNN在无信道知识情况下与信道状态感知MLSE具有竞争力。

Conclusion: RLIM码的ISI缓解优势并未因引入强神经检测器而消失；结合RLIM结构先验的训练掩码可显著提升紧凑SBRNN检测性能，并为无信道知识的高效MC接收机设计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Molecular communication (MC) suffers from severe diffusion memory because molecules released for one symbol may arrive during later symbols. Neural sequence detectors, especially sliding bidirectional recurrent neural networks (SBRNNs), can substantially outperform threshold detectors in such channels. This raises a central question for MC channel coding: does a code whose advantage was established under threshold detection retain it when both coded and uncoded transmission are evaluated with neural detection? This letter answers this question for run-length-limited ISI-mitigation (RLIM) codes, a class of constrained codes previously shown to provide large BER gains in MC. Across the tested operating points, the best RLIM-SBRNN receiver beats the best uncoded receiver, chosen between threshold and SBRNN detection, in $46$ of $59$ cases, with a mean gain of $10.36\times$ over those wins. We also propose an RLIM-tailored training mask for compact SBRNN detectors, improving the unmasked RLIM-SBRNN in $227$ of $236$ comparisons with $3.267\times$ mean gain when masking is beneficial. Finally, the compact masked RLIM-SBRNN is competitive with channel-state-aware MLSE despite using no channel knowledge.

</details>


### [231] [The Capacity Region for Classes of Sum-Broadcast Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12839)
*Amin Gohari,Yi Liu,Chandra Nair*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文求解了一类由多个广播信道求和构成的信道模型的容量区域；当各组成信道属于退化、less-noisy、more-capable、确定性或半确定性等类别时，辅助接收机外界与 Marton 内界相匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广播信道容量区域通常难以刻画。已有结果仅覆盖两个反向退化广播信道之和等特殊情形，因此本文希望将可精确求容量区域的范围扩展到更广泛的广播信道组合。

Method: 作者利用先前提出的辅助接收机外界，并证明其在所考虑的信道类别之和上与 Marton 可达内界一致；同时定义“primary broadcast channels”类，并对其求和情形建立类似匹配结果。

Result: 得到了由退化、less-noisy、more-capable、确定性、半确定性等组成信道构成的广播信道和的容量区域；推广了 El Gamal 1980 年关于两个反向退化广播信道之和的经典结果，并扩展到 primary broadcast channels 的求和模型。

Conclusion: 本文表明，对于若干结构化广播信道的组合，Marton 内界是紧的，辅助接收机外界可用于精确刻画容量区域，从而扩大了广播信道容量理论中可完全求解的模型类别。

Abstract: We compute the capacity region of a sum of broadcast channels whose components are degraded, less-noisy, more-capable, deterministic, or semi-deterministic. We achieve this by showing that an auxiliary-receiver outer bound, previously introduced by some of the authors, matches Marton's inner bound. This result generalizes a previously known result for the sum of two reversely degraded broadcast channels due to El Gamal (1980). Moreover, we define a class of primary broadcast channels and show an analogous result for the sum of primary broadcast channels.

</details>


### [232] [JSCGC: Joint Source-Channel-Generation Coding for Wireless Generative Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12858)
*Tong Wu,Zhiyong Chen,Guo Lu,Li Song,Feng Yang,Meixia Tao,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出联合源-信道-生成编码（JSCGC），用接收端生成模型替代传统解码器，将通信重构转为受信道观测条件控制的生成过程，以提升感知与语义质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分离式编码与学习式JSCC多基于率失真理论和通用失真度量，难以刻画复杂人类视觉感知，导致图像重建模糊或不真实。

Method: 作者提出JSCGC范式：在接收端使用生成模型，将接收信号作为条件来控制从学习到的条件分布中采样；目标从确定性失真最小化转为在感知约束下最大化互信息，并构建联合训练与高效随机采样框架，同时给出学习与推理阶段的理论分析。

Result: 在潜空间图像传输实验中，JSCGC在多种信道条件下持续提升基于特征、语义层面和分布层面的质量；其错误模式不同于传统失真，主要表现为语义不一致。

Conclusion: JSCGC为感知导向通信提供了生成式新范式，相比传统重构型通信更能产生逼真且语义质量更高的接收结果，但需要关注生成式采样带来的语义一致性风险。

Abstract: Conventional communication systems, including both separation-based coding and learning-based joint source-channel coding (JSCC), are typically designed under Shannon's rate-distortion theory. However, relying on generic distortion metrics fails to capture complex human visual perception, often resulting in blurred or unrealistic reconstructions. In this paper, we propose Joint Source-Channel-Generation Coding (JSCGC), a generative communication paradigm that replaces the conventional decoder with a generative model at the receiver. The received signal is treated as a condition that controls the sampling process into the learned conditional distribution, reformulating communication from deterministic reconstruction for distortion minimization to controlled generation for mutual information maximization under perceptual constraints. Based on this formulation, we develop a unified joint training and efficient stochastic sampling framework, and provide theoretical analysis of its effectiveness in both learning and inference stages. Extensive experiments on latent-space image transmission demonstrate that the JSCGC consistently improves feature-based, semantic-level, and distributional quality across diverse channel conditions, while exhibiting a distinct error behavior characterized by semantic inconsistency rather than distortion.

</details>


### [233] [Pinching-Antenna Enabled Multicell Wireless Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12888)
*Yunshu Chen,Qing Xue,Meng Hua,Bingpeng Zhou,Shaodan Ma*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究多波导夹持天线（PA）多小区通信系统，通过联合优化预编码、波导功率分配与天线位置来最大化加权和速率，所提交替优化方案显著优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PA 系统可通过动态调整介质波导上夹持单元的位置来重构无线传播环境，为提升无线系统性能提供新的空间自由度。多小区场景中小区间干扰强、变量耦合严重，使加权和速率最大化问题高度非凸，因此需要高效的联合优化方法。

Method: 论文构建多波导 PA 多小区通信系统的 WSR 最大化问题，联合优化预编码矩阵、波导功率分配和天线位置。方法上采用交替优化框架：先用分式规划引入辅助变量以解耦信号与干扰项；再用块坐标下降优化预编码与功率分配，得到闭式或半闭式更新；针对高维非凸的 PA 位置优化问题，使用粒子群优化进行搜索以提高可扩展性。

Result: 数值结果显示，在多种系统配置下，所提方案明显优于平均功率分配、固定天线位置、传统 MIMO 和 massive MIMO 等基线方案。

Conclusion: PA 系统通过可重构天线位置和联合资源优化能够有效缓解多小区干扰并提升系统加权和速率，展现出在大规模多小区无线通信中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Pinching antenna (PA) systems have recently emerged as a promising flexible-antenna technology, which can reconstruct the wireless propagation environment by dynamically adjusting the positions of pinching elements along dielectric waveguides, thereby providing new spatial degrees of freedom (DoFs) for enhancing wireless system performance. This paper investigates a multi-waveguide PA-based multi-cell communication system, focusing on the joint optimization of precoding matrices, waveguide power allocation, and antenna placement to maximize the weighted sum rate (WSR). In multi-cell scenarios, inter-cell interference typically leads to a highly coupled and nonconvex WSR maximization problem. To address this challenge, an efficient alternating optimization framework is adopted to optimize each variable in an iterative way. Specifically, fractional programming is first employed to reformulate the original problem by introducing auxiliary variables that decouple the signal and interference terms. Based on this reformulation, block coordinate descent is then applied to optimize the precoding matrices and power allocation, leading to closed-form or semi-closed-form updates. For the high-dimensional and nonconvex PA placement problem, particle swarm optimization (PSO) is utilized to perform an efficient search and improve scalability. Numerical results demonstrate that, under various system configurations, the proposed scheme significantly outperforms baseline methods, including average power allocation, fixed antenna placement, conventional multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO), and massive MIMO. These results highlight the strong potential of PA systems for large-scale multi-cell wireless communications.

</details>


### [234] [Bounds and Constructions of Maximum Toroidal Distance Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13008)
*Pengjie Zhong,Jinquan Luo,Yufeng Song*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究模 q 空间中的环面距离与最大环面距离（MTD）码，给出多类上界并构造若干在维度 2、4、8 上达到最优的显式 MTD 码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格基密码中编码消息与解密噪声都位于模 q 空间，因此需要理解该空间下的环面距离结构，并寻找具有尽可能大最小环面距离的最优码。

Method: 作者结合几何方法与线性规划方法，推导 Plotkin 型上界、局部球-Plotkin 上界和 Delsarte 线性规划上界；同时利用低维几何构造以及 E8 格等工具构造显式码。

Result: 论文证明若干上界在特定情形下是紧的；在维度 l=2 得到 Z_q^2 中四点 MTD 码族，在 l=4 给出一般构造及多个具体最优实例，在 l=8 通过 C=2mE8∩Z_q^8、q=4m 构造 MTD 码；在 l=16、q=4 时构造出最小环面距离为 3 的码，优于已知上界 2√3 所能直接刻画的情形。

Conclusion: 本文为模 q 环面空间中的最优距离码提供了理论上界与显式最优构造，尤其在维度 2、4、8 上给出明确 MTD 码，对格基密码中的编码与噪声容限分析具有潜在价值。

Abstract: In lattice-based cryptographic schemes, both encoded messages and accumulated decryption noise are represented in a modulo $q$ space. Therefore, it is natural to study toroidal distances and maximum toroidal distance (MTD) codes. In this paper, we derive some upper bounds for minimum toroidal distance of a code, including a Plotkin-type bound, a local ball--Plotkin bound, and a Delsarte linear programming bound. We also exhibit examples showing that these bounds are sharp in some cases. Moreover, we present several code constructions with good minimum distance, some of which are MTD codes. For $\ell=2$, we obtain a family of four-point MTD codes in $\mathbb Z_q^2$. For $\ell=4$, we propose a general code construction and exhibit several explicit instances for specific values of $q$, some of which are proven to be MTD codes. For $\ell=8$, using the $E_8$ lattice, we construct codes $C=2mE_8\cap \mathbb Z_q^8$, where $q=4m$ and show that they are MTD codes. These results give explicit optimal constructions of MTD codes for $\ell=2,4,8$. In the case $\ell=16$, we construct a code with minimum toroidal distance $3$ for $q=4$, while the known upper bound in this case is $2\sqrt{3}$. Our main tools are geometric and linear programming methods.

</details>


### [235] [Technical Supplement Report on Full-Duplex FBMC/QAM MIMO Systems: Transceiver Design and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13023)
*Sudhakar Rai,Prem Singh,Ekant Sharma,Aditya K. Jagannatham,Lajos Hanzo*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该技术报告研究FBMC/QAM多用户MISO系统的收发链路、有效信道建模、干扰来源、滤波器选择与功率优化，并通过仿真表明其在残余载波频偏下相较CP-OFDM具有误码率和网络频谱效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CP-OFDM在存在残余载波频偏等非理想同步条件时容易产生性能退化；FBMC体系具有更好的频谱局部化潜力，但FBMC/OQAM存在固有干扰和实现复杂性。本文旨在系统分析FBMC/QAM在多用户MISO场景中的端到端性能、干扰机制及资源优化方法。

Method: 论文构建了FBMC/QAM上行与下行完整信号处理链，刻画包含载波间干扰、符号间干扰、固有干扰与残余自干扰的端到端有效信道；从Balian-Low定理角度比较FBMC/QAM、CP-OFDM和FBMC/OQAM；分析PHYDYAS、Type-I、Type-II原型滤波器及其在MRT和ZF预编码下的干扰功率分解；提出在线随机逐次凸近似框架进行遍历和速率最大化，并给出闭式功率更新，同时与离线Monte Carlo方法对比。

Result: 仿真显示，在残余载波频偏条件下，FBMC/QAM相比CP-OFDM具有更优的误码率表现和网络频谱效率；不同原型滤波器及预编码方式会显著影响各类干扰分量和整体系统性能。

Conclusion: FBMC/QAM是多用户MISO系统中一种具有竞争力的多载波方案，尤其适合存在同步误差的场景；其性能依赖于原型滤波器设计、预编码策略和功率分配优化，在线随机优化方法可更高效地支持实际系统中的动态资源配置。

Abstract: This technical report presents the design and analysis of filter bank multicarrier (FBMC)/QAM multi-user MISO systems. We describe the complete uplink and downlink signal processing chains and characterize the end-to-end effective channel, including inter-carrier interference, inter-symbol interference, intrinsic interference, and residual self-interference. We compare FBMC/QAM with CP-OFDM and FBMC/OQAM through the lens of the Balian-Low theorem, and analyze prototype filter choices (PHYDYAS, Type-I, and Type-II), including the interference power breakdown under MRT and ZF precoding. Furthermore, we present an online stochastic successive convex approximation framework for ergodic sum-rate maximization with closed-form power updates, and contrast it with offline Monte Carlo-based approaches. Simulation results demonstrate the BER and network spectral efficiency advantages of FBMC/QAM over CP-OFDM under residual carrier frequency offset.

</details>


### [236] [Active Sensing-assisted UAV Communications with Jittering: Framework and Performance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13036)
*Guangji Chen,Long Shi,Qingqing Wu,Qiaoyan Peng,Caihong Kai*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向存在抖动的地面到无人机链路的两阶段主动感知辅助通信框架，通过感知获取到达角并优化通信波束，对比通信导向与感知导向两类方案，给出速率闭式表达、最优时间分配和性能判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蜂窝网络支撑无人机可靠通信对低空经济发展很关键，但无人机抖动会导致波束失准，从而显著降低通信质量。集成感知与通信为利用链路感知信息补偿抖动、提升地面到无人机通信性能提供了新思路。

Method: 论文设计两阶段框架：第一阶段用于辅助到达角估计，第二阶段基于估计角度进行通信。感知导向方案在第一阶段发送确定性信号以增强AoA获取，第二阶段纯通信；通信导向方案在两个阶段均发送高斯承载信息信号，并利用高斯随机信号进行AoA估计。作者为两类方案推导AoA最大似然估计器、Cramér-Rao下界、可达速率闭式表达，并以第一阶段时间分配为变量进行总速率优化，得到半闭式最优解。

Result: 分析揭示了两阶段中感知质量与通信质量之间的基本权衡；给出了最大化总体速率的最优时间分配；进一步推导出通信导向方案优于感知导向方案的充分条件，该条件呈现阈值结构。渐近分析表明，在高发射功率区域，两类方案相对无抖动上界的性能损失趋近于零。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，主动感知辅助通信可有效缓解无人机抖动引起的波束失准问题；通过合理选择信号设计与阶段时间分配，系统能够在感知精度和通信速率之间取得优化平衡，并在高功率条件下接近理想无抖动性能。

Abstract: Providing reliable communication for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) via existing cellular networks is crucial for enabling the rapid growth of the low-altitude economy. However, UAV jittering significantly degrades communication quality due to induced beam misalignment. Inspired by recent advances in integrated sensing and communication, we propose a novel two-stage active sensing-assisted communication framework tailored for ground-to-UAV links with jittering. Specifically, two schemes are conceived to leverage sensing for enhancing communication performance, namely the communication-oriented scheme and the sensing-oriented scheme. For the sensing-oriented scheme, deterministic signals are employed in the first stage to facilitate angle-of-arrival (AoA) acquisition at the UAV side, followed by pure communication service in the second stage by using the estimated AoA. In contrast, the communication-oriented scheme employs Gaussian information-bearing signals throughout both stages, with AoA estimation relying on Gaussian random signals. For both schemes, we provide maximum likelihood estimators for AoA, along with analytical results characterizing the Cramér-Rao bound. To capture the performance limit, closed-form expressions for the achievable rates of the two schemes are derived, unveiling a fundamental tradeoff between sensing and communication quality across the two stages by tuning the time allocated to the first stage. The optimal time allocation that maximizes the overall rate is obtained in semi-closed-form. Based on these results, we unveil a sufficient condition under which the communication-oriented scheme outperforms the sensing-oriented scheme, which admits an interesting threshold-based structure. Asymptotic analysis demonstrates that the performance loss of the proposed schemes relative to the jitter-free upper bound approaches zero in the high transmit power regime.

</details>


### [237] [Polar Decoding Tree Pruning Based on Soft Output Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13214)
*Li Shen,Yongpeng Wu,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出基于软输出信息的剪枝 SCL 极化码译码器，在保持纠错性能不变的同时显著降低排序与计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 SCL 译码虽然性能优秀，但列表中保留了许多对最终判决贡献极小的路径，导致排序开销和计算复杂度较高；因此需要一种更准确、低代价的路径剪枝机制。

Method: 作者利用 soft-output SCL 与 soft-output fast SCL 中的分块软输出提取过程，近似估计每条译码路径为正确路径的概率，并依据预设可靠性阈值剪除低可靠路径，形成 SOP-SCL 及其快速版本 SOP-FSCL。

Result: 所提方法显著降低译码复杂度，并且不牺牲纠错性能；同时相比已有先进的剪枝极化码译码器具有更高效率。

Conclusion: 基于软输出概率估计的路径剪枝是一种有效的 SCL 复杂度优化策略，可在保持误码性能的前提下减少无效路径处理，适用于常规和快速 SCL 架构。

Abstract: Although the successive cancellation list (SCL) decoding of polar codes exhibits excellent performance, it retains many decoding paths in the list with negligible contribution to the final output, resulting in high sorting and computational complexity. In this letter, we propose a novel pruning strategy to mitigate the decoding complexity. By leveraging the blockwise soft output extraction process of soft-output SCL and soft-output fast SCL decoding, we provide an accurate approximation of the probability that a decoding path is correct, and thus accordingly prune the paths failing to meet a pre-defined reliability threshold. The complexity reduction achieved by the proposed soft-output-based pruned SCL (SOP-SCL) decoder and its fast version, SOP-FSCL decoder, is significant, without any compromise in error-correction performance. Meanwhile, they also prove to be more efficient than state-of-the-art pruned polar decoders.

</details>


### [238] [A $q$-analogue of the rational normal curve and linearized Reed-Solomon codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13246)
*Valentina Astore,Martino Borello,Alain Couvreur,Flavio Salizzoni*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文为线性化 Reed-Solomon 码建立了基于 q-类比有理正规曲线的几何框架，给出若干参数下的几何刻画，并通过 Hilbert 函数与超曲面条件揭示其结构性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hamming 度量中的线性码与射影代数簇之间已有成熟联系，而 sum-rank 度量及线性化 Reed-Solomon 码虽在多个应用场景中重要，但其结构、几何性质和可区分特征仍理解不足。

Method: 作者引入有理正规曲线的 q-类比来描述线性化 Reed-Solomon 码，扩展基于 Schur 乘积的几何/代数技术到 sum-rank 度量，并研究相关坐标环的 Hilbert 函数与正则性。

Result: 在特定参数选择下得到几何刻画，发现对应点集满足异常多的 q+1 次超曲面条件，并详细描述了 Hilbert 函数行为及其 regularity。

Conclusion: 该工作将代数几何视角系统引入线性化 Reed-Solomon 码的研究，提供了新的结构性不变量和潜在区分工具，同时也加深了对 Gabidulin 码相关几何性质的理解。

Abstract: The relationship between linear codes in the Hamming metric and projective algebraic varieties has led to deep interactions between coding theory and algebraic geometry, with classical examples such as Reed-Solomon codes and the rational normal curve. On the other hand, the sum-rank metric has recently gained attention due to applications in network coding, distributed storage, and post-quantum cryptography, with linearized Reed-Solomon codes emerging as optimal constructions. Despite recent advances, their structural and geometric properties are still not fully understood, and existing distinguishers remain limited. In this paper, we develop a geometric framework for linearized Reed-Solomon codes by considering a $q$-analogue of the rational normal curve. This yields a geometric characterization for certain parameter choices and reveals that the corresponding sets of points satisfy unexpectedly many $(q+1)$-degree hypersurface conditions. Our approach extends Schur-product-based techniques from the Hamming and rank-metric settings to the sum-rank metric case. Finally, we study the Hilbert function of the associated coordinate ring, providing a detailed description of its behavior and identifying its regularity, which also sheds new light on Gabidulin codes.

</details>


### [239] [Error Probability Analysis of Quantum Communication with Phase-squeezed M-PSK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13286)
*Nikos A. Mitsiou,Ioannis Krikidis*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究相位压缩态 M-PSK 在自适应 Mark-II 相位测量接收机下的符号错误概率，并提出高效近似分析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: M-PSK 的关键信息承载于光学相位，因此需要采用物理可实现的相位测量模型来准确评估相位压缩对量子通信误码性能与光子效率的提升。

Method: 基于 Mark-II 接收机在 Fock 基下的相位概率算子测度进行理论分析；进一步提出两种 SEP 计算方法：一种通过压缩态噪声诱导的相位概率密度并与 Mark-II 相位不确定性进行角卷积；另一种使用有效切向方差模型，将 SEP 表达为含 Owen's T 函数的闭式形式。

Result: 数值结果表明，相位压缩相比相干态传输可显著降低 M-PSK 的 SEP，且星座阶数越高收益越明显；在所研究场景中，随平均发射光子数增加，压缩几乎可使 M-PSK 的光子效率翻倍。提出的近似方法与 Mark-II POM 分析吻合良好，通常误差约为 2–4 个光子。

Conclusion: 相位压缩结合 Mark-II 自适应相位测量能够有效提升量子 M-PSK 通信性能；所提出的角卷积与有效切向方差近似为分析相位压缩 M-PSK 的 SEP 提供了准确且计算高效的工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the symbol error probability (SEP) of phase-squeezed M-ary phase-shift keying (M-PSK). Since the relevant observable for M-PSK detection is the optical phase, we adopt the adaptive Mark-II receiver which is a physically realizable phase measurement. First, we develop a theoretical analysis based on the phase probability operator measure (POM) of the Mark-II scheme in the Fock basis. Then, we develop two SEP methods based on the statistics of the received PSK symbol and the error introduced by the Mark-II measurement. The first method derives the phase probability density induced by the squeezed state noise and incorporates the additional Mark-II phase uncertainty through an angular convolution. Since this convolution does not admit a simple closed form, we also introduce an effective tangential-variance model, which yields a closed form SEP expression in terms of the Owen's T-function. Numerical results show that phase squeezing substantially reduces the SEP of M-PSK compared to coherent state transmission, with greater gains for higher constellation orders. Notably, for the investigated scenario, squeezing can almost double the photon efficiency of M-PSK as the mean number of transmitted photons increases. Finally, the proposed approximations closely follow the Mark-II POM analysis, typically within an accuracy of 2-4 photons, and therefore provide accurate and computationally efficient tools for analyzing phase squeezed quantum M-PSK communication.

</details>


### [240] [$W-δ-μ$ dual codes and LCD codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13467)
*Avanish Kumar Chaturvedi,Satyadeep Pandey*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文在有限域向量空间 F_q^n 上提出一种新的乘积形式，统一并推广欧氏、Hermitian 与 δ 乘积，并研究其诱导的对偶码及相关编码性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典编码理论中，不同内积会导出不同的对偶码、自正交码、自对偶码、含对偶码和 LCD 码。已有欧氏、Hermitian、δ 等乘积各自适用于不同场景，因此需要一个更一般的框架来统一这些结构并扩展到更广泛的代数环境。

Method: 作者定义新的广义乘积，推导该乘积下对偶码的一般性质，建立其与欧氏对偶的关系；进一步给出自正交、自对偶、含对偶和 LCD 码的定义、刻画与存在条件；并计算重复码、二元码、λ-常循环码等若干典型码类在该乘积下的对偶码，最后将该乘积推广到半单环上的码。

Result: 论文获得了广义乘积下对偶码的结构性质与判定准则，明确了多类重要线性码在该乘积意义下的对偶形式，并展示该理论可自然覆盖已有乘积及其对应结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为有限域及半单环上线性码的对偶理论提供了统一框架，有助于系统研究自正交、自对偶、含对偶和 LCD 等重要码类，并扩展了经典乘积理论的适用范围。

Abstract: We introduce a new product on the ambient space $F_q^n$ as a generalization of Euclidean, Hermitian and $δ$ products. We give some general properties of the dual codes, relation with Euclidean duals, definition and characterization of self orthogonal, self dual, dual containing and LCD codes along with certain existence conditions. Also, we calculate the dual codes of some classes of codes like repetition, binary and $λ$-constacyclic codes with respect to this product. Further, we extend and analyse this notion of the product for codes over semisimple rings.

</details>


### [241] [Fundamental Limits of Hypergraph Edge Partitioning under Independent Edge Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13491)
*Javad Maheri,K. K. Krishnan Namboodiri,Petros Elia*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究超图边划分的理论极限，给出适用于广泛独立边概率模型的下界，并提出达到该下界常数因子内的确定性划分算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超图边划分在分布式计算、通信、优化和机器学习中很重要；核心目标是在将超边分配到多个分区时最小化每个分区涉及的顶点数，即 vertex footprint。现有工作缺少对广泛随机超图模型下所有可能算法的统一最优性界定。

Method: 作者考虑每条超边可按自身概率独立出现的通用概率超图模型，并结合一个确定性划分器与新的 converse 下界证明，分析任意算法在给定超边规模条件下可达到的最小 footprint。

Result: 在温和条件 N ≤ C(floor(sqrt(nd/2)), d) 且 |X| ≳ nN log N 时，以至少 1-2/(3n^z) 的概率，任何算法的 footprint 都不能低于 π*_X = (1/(2√2)) n / N^{1/d}；提出的划分器对每个 X 都能达到该极限的一个小常数因子内，并实现近似均衡的超边分配。

Conclusion: 该工作刻画了广泛随机超图模型中边划分问题的基本极限，证明了所提算法在稠密和稀疏超图（甚至规模低至关于 n 线性）上均具有近似最优的 vertex footprint 与负载均衡性质。

Abstract: Hypergraph edge partitioning is a central problem in theoretical and applied computer science, with broad impact on distributed computation, communications, optimization, and machine learning. In this setting, one is given a collection of hyperedges -- each consisting of up to $d$ vertices from a ground set of size $n$ -- and seeks to assign these hyperedges across $N$ partitions so as to minimize, for example, the vertex footprint, i.e., the maximum number of vertices that appear in any partition. We here identify the fundamental limits of hypergraph edge partitioning -- optimized over all conceivable algorithms -- for a broad class of probabilistic hypergraph models where each hyperedge may appear independently with \emph{its own} probability; a model sufficiently general to encompass well-known models such as the Degree-Corrected or Mixed-Membership models, the Hypergraph Stochastic Block model, the Latent-Space/Geometric or Kernel Models, and others. By pairing our deterministic partitioner with a new converse, we first show that, for any $n,d$, and under the very mild condition of $N \leq \binom{\lfloor\sqrt{\frac{nd}{2}}\rfloor}{d}$, as long as the hyperedge set $\mathbf{X}$ satisfies $|\mathbf{X}| \gtrsim n N \log N$, then with probability at least $1-2/3n^z$, no algorithm can provide a footprint $π_{\mathbf{X}}$ less than $$π^{\bigstar}_{\mathbf{X}} = \frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}\frac{n}{N^{1/d}}. $$ We then show that our hypergraph partitioner comes to within a small constant factor from $π^{\bigstar}_{\mathbf{X}}$, for each $\mathbf{X}$. This optimality captures dense and sparse hypergraphs alike (with sizes down to linear in $n$), and it additionally entails a near-optimally balanced allocation of hyperedges across partitions.

</details>
